$Keyboard US
$UnitSystem SI C kPa kJ mass deg

{OTEC NACALA - MODELO PRINCIPAL, PARTES 1 A 17B
 Estado NH3: 1 saida condensador; 2 saida bomba; 3 entrada turbina;
 4 saida turbina. Interfaces das funcoes numericas; unidades repostas no main.
 Referencia: 10 MW liquidos apos reserva orcamental de 500 kW.
 Cenario 14: 108 modulos por PHE; U bifasicos prescritos=3.6 kW/(m2 K).
 Geometria de cenario; perfil de densidade sintetico; perdas NH3 omitidas.
 TEOS 15/16: SA quente=34.986434046 e fria=35.005680627 g/kg;
 p=0 dbar, T=0..35 C. Salinidades fixas nos ajustes.
 Parte 16: exergia do nucleo termico; sem hidraulica completa nem mistura.
 Parte 17B: caudal NH3 nominal, equipamento fixo, eficiencias constantes;
 controlo termico idealizado. Menor auxiliar entre modos admissiveis.
 Modos: 1 MH/MC nominais; 2 MH/Tc nominais; 3 Te/MC nominais;
 4 Te/Tc nominais. Indice seleccionado 0: nenhum modo admissivel.
 Os cenarios de temperatura nao constituem uma serie mensal observada.}

FUNCTION OTEC_fF(ReM,betaM)
  $VarInfo ReM Units='-'
  $VarInfo betaM Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_fF Units='-'
  IF (ReM<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Martin: Reynolds deve ser positivo. Valor XXX',ReM)
  IF (betaM<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Martin: angulo deve ser positivo. Valor XXX',betaM)
  IF (betaM>80) THEN CALL ERROR('Martin: angulo superior a 80 graus. Valor XXX',betaM)
  IF (ReM<2000) THEN
    f0:=16/ReM
    f1:=149/ReM+0.9625
  ELSE
    f0:=(1.56*LN(ReM)-3)^(-2)
    f1:=9.75/ReM^0.289
  ENDIF
  radA:=0.045*TAN(betaM*1 [deg])+0.09*SIN(betaM*1 [deg])+f0/COS(betaM*1 [deg])
  radB:=3.8*f1
  IF (radA<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Martin: primeiro argumento de raiz invalido XXX',radA)
  IF (radB<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Martin: segundo argumento de raiz invalido XXX',radB)
  zz:=COS(betaM*1 [deg])/SQRT(radA)+(1-COS(betaM*1 [deg]))/SQRT(radB)
  OTEC_fF:=1/zz^2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_Nu(ReN,PrN,betaN)
  $VarInfo ReN Units='-'
  $VarInfo PrN Units='-'
  $VarInfo betaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Nu Units='-'
  IF (PrN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Nusselt: Prandtl deve ser positivo. Valor XXX',PrN)
  IF (betaN<10) THEN CALL ERROR('Nusselt: angulo abaixo de 10 graus. Valor XXX',betaN)
  ffN:=OTEC_fF(ReN,betaN)
  termN:=ffN*ReN^2*SIN(2*betaN*1 [deg])
  IF (termN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Nusselt: argumento da potencia invalido XXX',termN)
  OTEC_Nu:=0.205*PrN^(1/3)*termN^0.374
END

FUNCTION OTEC_h(Gx,Dhx,mux,kx,cpx,betax)
  $VarInfo Gx Units='-'
  $VarInfo Dhx Units='-'
  $VarInfo mux Units='-'
  $VarInfo kx Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpx Units='-'
  $VarInfo betax Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_h Units='-'
  IF (Gx<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Conveccao: fluxo massico invalido XXX',Gx)
  IF (Dhx<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Conveccao: diametro invalido XXX',Dhx)
  IF (mux<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Conveccao: viscosidade invalida XXX',mux)
  IF (kx<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Conveccao: condutividade invalida XXX',kx)
  IF (cpx<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Conveccao: calor especifico invalido XXX',cpx)
  rex:=Gx*Dhx/mux
  prx:=cpx*mux/kx
  OTEC_h:=OTEC_Nu(rex,prx,betax)*kx/Dhx
END

FUNCTION OTEC_Ucalc(Ux,CpR,Rb,Nv,Ap,Aw,Aa,Dw,Da,rm,muw,kw,cpw,mua,ka,cpa,Rt,betau)
  $VarInfo Ux Units='-'
  $VarInfo CpR Units='-'
  $VarInfo Rb Units='-'
  $VarInfo Nv Units='-'
  $VarInfo Ap Units='-'
  $VarInfo Aw Units='-'
  $VarInfo Aa Units='-'
  $VarInfo Dw Units='-'
  $VarInfo Da Units='-'
  $VarInfo rm Units='-'
  $VarInfo muw Units='-'
  $VarInfo mua Units='-'
  $VarInfo kw Units='-'
  $VarInfo ka Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpw Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpa Units='-'
  $VarInfo Rt Units='-'
  $VarInfo betau Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Ucalc Units='-'
  IF (Ux<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: valor de tentativa nao positivo XXX',Ux)
  IF (CpR<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: carga de pre-aquecimento invalida XXX',CpR)
  IF (Rb<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: area da vaporizacao invalida XXX',Rb)
  IF (Nv<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: canais de velocidade invalidos XXX',Nv)
  IF (Ap<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: area de placa invalida XXX',Ap)
  IF (Aw<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: canal de agua invalido XXX',Aw)
  IF (Aa<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: canal de amonia invalido XXX',Aa)
  IF (Rt<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: resistencia termica negativa XXX',Rt)
  areaR:=Rb+CpR/Ux
  ncR:=MAX(areaR/(2*Ap),Nv)
  gwR:=rm/(ncR*Aw)
  gaR:=1/(ncR*Aa)
  hwR:=OTEC_h(gwR,Dw,muw,kw,cpw,betau)
  haR:=OTEC_h(gaR,Da,mua,ka,cpa,betau)
  OTEC_Ucalc:=1/(1/hwR+Rt+1/haR)
END

FUNCTION OTEC_Usolve(CpR,Rb,Nv,Ap,Aw,Aa,Dw,Da,rm,muw,kw,cpw,mua,ka,cpa,Rt,betau)
  $VarInfo CpR Units='-'
  $VarInfo Rb Units='-'
  $VarInfo Nv Units='-'
  $VarInfo Ap Units='-'
  $VarInfo Aw Units='-'
  $VarInfo Aa Units='-'
  $VarInfo Dw Units='-'
  $VarInfo Da Units='-'
  $VarInfo rm Units='-'
  $VarInfo muw Units='-'
  $VarInfo mua Units='-'
  $VarInfo kw Units='-'
  $VarInfo ka Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpw Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpa Units='-'
  $VarInfo Rt Units='-'
  $VarInfo betau Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Usolve Units='-'
  loU:=50
  hiU:=3500
  floU:=loU-OTEC_Ucalc(loU,CpR,Rb,Nv,Ap,Aw,Aa,Dw,Da,rm,muw,kw,cpw,mua,ka,cpa,Rt,betau)
  fhiU:=hiU-OTEC_Ucalc(hiU,CpR,Rb,Nv,Ap,Aw,Aa,Dw,Da,rm,muw,kw,cpw,mua,ka,cpa,Rt,betau)
  IF (floU=0) THEN
    OTEC_Usolve:=loU
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  IF (fhiU=0) THEN
    OTEC_Usolve:=hiU
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  IF (floU*fhiU>0) THEN CALL ERROR('Fecho U: raiz nao delimitada entre 50 e 3500 W/m2K.')
  iterU:=0
  REPEAT
    midU:=(loU+hiU)/2
    fmidU:=midU-OTEC_Ucalc(midU,CpR,Rb,Nv,Ap,Aw,Aa,Dw,Da,rm,muw,kw,cpw,mua,ka,cpa,Rt,betau)
    IF (floU*fmidU<=0) THEN
      hiU:=midU
      fhiU:=fmidU
    ELSE
      loU:=midU
      floU:=fmidU
    ENDIF
    iterU:=iterU+1
  UNTIL (iterU>=60)
  OTEC_Usolve:=(loU+hiU)/2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_muSW_tubo(TcN,SpN)
  $VarInfo TcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo SpN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_muSW_tubo Units='-'
  {Viscosidade: T em C numericos; SP pratica; retorno Pa s. Sharqawy (2010).}
  IF (TcN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Viscosidade: temperatura abaixo do dominio XXX',TcN)
  IF (TcN>180) THEN CALL ERROR('Viscosidade: temperatura acima do dominio XXX',TcN)
  IF (SpN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Viscosidade: salinidade pratica negativa XXX',SpN)
  sN:=35.16504*SpN/(35*1000)
  IF (sN>0.15) THEN CALL ERROR('Viscosidade: salinidade fora do dominio XXX',sN)
  aN:=1.540913604+0.019981117208*TcN-0.000095203865864*TcN^2
  bN:=7.9739318223-0.075614568881*TcN+0.00047237011074*TcN^2
  mwN:=0.000042844324477+1/(0.15700386464*(TcN+64.99262005)^2-91.296496657)
  OTEC_muSW_tubo:=mwN*(1+aN*sN+bN*sN^2)
END

FUNCTION OTEC_fD_tubo(ReT,epsT)
  $VarInfo ReT Units='-'
  $VarInfo epsT Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_fD_tubo Units='-'
  {Darcy: 64/Re laminar; Colebrook turbulento; transicao 2300..4000 rejeitada.}
  IF (ReT<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Tubagem: Reynolds deve ser positivo XXX',ReT)
  IF (epsT<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Tubagem: rugosidade relativa negativa XXX',epsT)
  IF (epsT>=0.1) THEN CALL ERROR('Tubagem: rugosidade relativa fora do modelo XXX',epsT)
  IF (ReT<2300) THEN
    OTEC_fD_tubo:=64/ReT
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  IF (ReT<4000) THEN CALL ERROR('Tubagem: regime de transicao requer tratamento proprio. Re=XXX',ReT)
  xlT:=1
  xhT:=100
  flT:=xlT+2*LOG10(epsT/3.7+2.51*xlT/ReT)
  fhT:=xhT+2*LOG10(epsT/3.7+2.51*xhT/ReT)
  IF (flT*fhT>=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Colebrook: raiz fora do intervalo numerico.')
  itT:=0
  REPEAT
    xmT:=(xlT+xhT)/2
    fmT:=xmT+2*LOG10(epsT/3.7+2.51*xmT/ReT)
    IF (flT*fmT<=0) THEN
      xhT:=xmT
    ELSE
      xlT:=xmT
      flT:=fmT
    ENDIF
    itT:=itT+1
  UNTIL (itT>=60)
  OTEC_fD_tubo:=1/((xlT+xhT)/2)^2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_Ptubo_num(mN,rVN,nN,aN,dN,lN,rhoN,muN,epsN,etaN)
{Potencia electrica total kW; nN ramos paralelos, lN de um ramo.}
  $VarInfo mN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rVN Units='-'
  $VarInfo nN Units='-'
  $VarInfo aN Units='-'
  $VarInfo dN Units='-'
  $VarInfo lN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhoN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muN Units='-'
  $VarInfo epsN Units='-'
  $VarInfo etaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Ptubo_num Units='-'
  IF (mN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: caudal de amonia deve ser positivo XXX',mN)
  IF (nN<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: numero de ramos invalido XXX',nN)
  IF (rVN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: razao volumetrica invalida XXX',rVN)
  IF (aN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: area de tubagem invalida XXX',aN)
  IF (dN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: diametro invalido XXX',dN)
  IF (lN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: comprimento negativo XXX',lN)
  IF (rhoN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: densidade invalida XXX',rhoN)
  IF (muN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: viscosidade invalida XXX',muN)
  IF (etaN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: eficiencia invalida XXX',etaN)
  IF (etaN>1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: eficiencia superior a um XXX',etaN)
  vvN:=mN*rVN/(nN*aN)
  reN:=rhoN*vvN*dN/muN
  fdN:=OTEC_fD_tubo(reN,epsN)
  dpN:=fdN*(lN/dN)*rhoN*vvN^2/2
  OTEC_Ptubo_num:=dpN*(mN*rVN)/etaN/1000
END

FUNCTION OTEC_mparcial(Pn,wn,mmax,rvC,rvH,nC,nH,aC,aH,dC,dH,lC,lH,rhC,rhH,muC,muH,epC,epH,etC,etH)

  $VarInfo Pn Units='-'
  $VarInfo wn Units='-'
  $VarInfo mmax Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvH Units='-'
  $VarInfo nC Units='-'
  $VarInfo nH Units='-'
  $VarInfo aC Units='-'
  $VarInfo aH Units='-'
  $VarInfo dC Units='-'
  $VarInfo dH Units='-'
  $VarInfo lC Units='-'
  $VarInfo lH Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhH Units='-'
  $VarInfo muC Units='-'
  $VarInfo muH Units='-'
  $VarInfo epC Units='-'
  $VarInfo epH Units='-'
  $VarInfo etC Units='-'
  $VarInfo etH Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_mparcial Units='-'
  IF (Pn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: meta de potencia invalida XXX',Pn)
  IF (wn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: trabalho disponivel nao positivo XXX',wn)
  mlN:=Pn/wn
  mhN:=mmax
  IF (mhN<=mlN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: capacidade tubular insuficiente para a meta.')
  phN:=mhN*wn-OTEC_Ptubo_num(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC)-OTEC_Ptubo_num(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH)
  IF (phN<Pn) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: meta nao atingida no intervalo admissivel; rever ramos e tracado.')
  
  rcN:=rhC*(mlN*rvC/(nC*aC))*dC/muC
  rhN:=rhH*(mlN*rvH/(nH*aH))*dH/muH
  IF (rcN<4000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: intervalo frio nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rcN)
  IF (rhN<4000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: intervalo quente nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rhN)
  ppN:=OTEC_Ptubo_num(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC)+OTEC_Ptubo_num(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH)
  IF (wn<=3*ppN/mhN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: monotonia nao garantida; requer procura de ramo propria.')
  iterN:=0
  REPEAT
    mmN:=(mlN+mhN)/2
    pmN:=mmN*wn-OTEC_Ptubo_num(mmN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC)-OTEC_Ptubo_num(mmN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH)
    IF (pmN>=Pn) THEN
      mhN:=mmN
    ELSE
      mlN:=mmN
    ENDIF
    iterN:=iterN+1
  UNTIL (iterN>=60)
  OTEC_mparcial:=(mlN+mhN)/2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_colfrac11(exN)
  $VarInfo exN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_colfrac11 Units='-'
  IF (exN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 11: expoente de perfil deve ser positivo XXX',exN)
  OTEC_colfrac11:=exN/(exN+1)
END

FUNCTION OTEC_Prede11(mN,rVN,nN,aN,dN,lN,rhoN,muN,epsN,etaN,kN)
  $VarInfo mN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rVN Units='-'
  $VarInfo nN Units='-'
  $VarInfo aN Units='-'
  $VarInfo dN Units='-'
  $VarInfo lN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhoN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muN Units='-'
  $VarInfo epsN Units='-'
  $VarInfo etaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Prede11 Units='-'
  IF (kN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 11: K negativo XXX',kN)
  
  ppN:=OTEC_Ptubo_num(mN,rVN,nN,aN,dN,lN,rhoN,muN,epsN,etaN)
  vvN:=mN*rVN/(nN*aN)
  OTEC_Prede11:=ppN+kN*rhoN*vvN^2/2*(mN*rVN)/etaN/1000
END

FUNCTION OTEC_mrede11(Pn,wn,mmax,rvC,rvH,nC,nH,aC,aH,dC,dH,lC,lH,rhC,rhH,muC,muH,epC,epH,etC,etH,kC,kH)

  $VarInfo kC Units='-'
  $VarInfo kH Units='-'
  $VarInfo Pn Units='-'
  $VarInfo wn Units='-'
  $VarInfo mmax Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvH Units='-'
  $VarInfo nC Units='-'
  $VarInfo nH Units='-'
  $VarInfo aC Units='-'
  $VarInfo aH Units='-'
  $VarInfo dC Units='-'
  $VarInfo dH Units='-'
  $VarInfo lC Units='-'
  $VarInfo lH Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhH Units='-'
  $VarInfo muC Units='-'
  $VarInfo muH Units='-'
  $VarInfo epC Units='-'
  $VarInfo epH Units='-'
  $VarInfo etC Units='-'
  $VarInfo etH Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_mrede11 Units='-'
  IF (Pn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: meta de potencia invalida XXX',Pn)
  IF (wn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: trabalho disponivel nao positivo XXX',wn)
  mlN:=Pn/wn
  mhN:=mmax
  IF (mhN<=mlN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: capacidade tubular insuficiente para a meta.')
  phN:=mhN*wn-OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)-OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)
  IF (phN<Pn) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: meta nao atingida no intervalo admissivel; rever ramos e tracado.')
  
  rcN:=rhC*(mlN*rvC/(nC*aC))*dC/muC
  rhN:=rhH*(mlN*rvH/(nH*aH))*dH/muH
  IF (rcN<4000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: intervalo frio nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rcN)
  IF (rhN<4000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: intervalo quente nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rhN)
  ppN:=OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)+OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)
  IF (wn<=3*ppN/mhN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: monotonia nao garantida; requer procura de ramo propria.')
  iterN:=0
  REPEAT
    mmN:=(mlN+mhN)/2
    pmN:=mmN*wn-OTEC_Prede11(mmN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)-OTEC_Prede11(mmN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)
    IF (pmN>=Pn) THEN
      mhN:=mmN
    ELSE
      mlN:=mmN
    ENDIF
    iterN:=iterN+1
  UNTIL (iterN>=60)
  OTEC_mrede11:=(mlN+mhN)/2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_Pcan12(mN,rmN,ncN,aN,dhN,lN,rhoN,muN,beN,etaN)
  $VarInfo mN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rmN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ncN Units='-'
  $VarInfo aN Units='-'
  $VarInfo dhN Units='-'
  $VarInfo lN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhoN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muN Units='-'
  $VarInfo beN Units='-'
  $VarInfo etaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Pcan12 Units='-'
  IF (mN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: caudal nao positivo XXX',mN)
  IF (rmN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: razao de caudais invalida XXX',rmN)
  IF (ncN<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: canais insuficientes XXX',ncN)
  IF (aN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: area de canal invalida XXX',aN)
  IF (dhN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: diametro de canal invalido XXX',dhN)
  IF (lN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: comprimento de canal invalido XXX',lN)
  IF (rhoN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: densidade invalida XXX',rhoN)
  IF (muN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: viscosidade invalida XXX',muN)
  IF (etaN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: eficiencia invalida XXX',etaN)
  IF (etaN>1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: eficiencia superior a um XXX',etaN)
  ggN:=mN*rmN/(ncN*aN)
  reN:=ggN*dhN/muN
  ffN:=OTEC_fF(reN,beN)
  dpN:=4*ffN*(lN/dhN)*ggN^2/(2*rhoN)
  OTEC_Pcan12:=dpN*(mN*rmN/rhoN)/etaN/1000
END

FUNCTION OTEC_check12(feN,fcN,vcN,vhN,vweN,vwcN,rrN)
  $VarInfo feN Units='-'
  $VarInfo fcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo vcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo vhN Units='-'
  $VarInfo vweN Units='-'
  $VarInfo vwcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rrN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_check12 Units='-'
  IF (feN<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: area do evaporador insuficiente; aumentar modulos XXX',feN)
  IF (fcN<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: area do condensador insuficiente; aumentar modulos XXX',fcN)
  IF (vcN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: velocidade de captacao fria excedida XXX',vcN)
  IF (vhN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: velocidade de captacao quente excedida XXX',vhN)
  IF (vweN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: velocidade nos canais quentes excedida XXX',vweN)
  IF (vwcN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: velocidade nos canais frios excedida XXX',vwcN)
  IF (ABS(rrN)>0.001) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: residuo de potencia acima de 0.001 kW XXX',rrN)
  OTEC_check12:=1
END

FUNCTION OTEC_mdisc12(Pn,wn,mmax,rvC,rvH,nC,nH,aC,aH,dC,dH,lC,lH,rhC,rhH,muC,muH,epC,epH,etC,etH,kC,kH,nEc,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rmE,rmC,rhE,rhCo,muE,muCo,bet)

  $VarInfo kC Units='-'
  $VarInfo kH Units='-'
  $VarInfo nEc Units='-'
  $VarInfo nCc Units='-'
  $VarInfo acw Units='-'
  $VarInfo dhw Units='-'
  $VarInfo lp Units='-'
  $VarInfo rmE Units='-'
  $VarInfo rmC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhE Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhCo Units='-'
  $VarInfo muE Units='-'
  $VarInfo muCo Units='-'
  $VarInfo bet Units='-'
  $VarInfo Pn Units='-'
  $VarInfo wn Units='-'
  $VarInfo mmax Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvH Units='-'
  $VarInfo nC Units='-'
  $VarInfo nH Units='-'
  $VarInfo aC Units='-'
  $VarInfo aH Units='-'
  $VarInfo dC Units='-'
  $VarInfo dH Units='-'
  $VarInfo lC Units='-'
  $VarInfo lH Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhH Units='-'
  $VarInfo muC Units='-'
  $VarInfo muH Units='-'
  $VarInfo epC Units='-'
  $VarInfo epH Units='-'
  $VarInfo etC Units='-'
  $VarInfo etH Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_mdisc12 Units='-'
  IF (Pn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: meta de potencia invalida XXX',Pn)
  IF (wn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: trabalho disponivel nao positivo XXX',wn)
  mlN:=Pn/wn
  mhN:=mmax
  IF (mhN<=mlN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: capacidade tubular insuficiente para a meta.')
  phN:=mhN*wn-OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)-OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmE,nEc,acw,dhw,lp,rhE,muE,bet,etH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmC,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rhCo,muCo,bet,etC)
  IF (phN<Pn) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: meta nao atingida no intervalo admissivel; rever ramos e tracado.')
  
  rcN:=rhC*(mlN*rvC/(nC*aC))*dC/muC
  rhN:=rhH*(mlN*rvH/(nH*aH))*dH/muH
  IF (rcN<4000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: intervalo frio nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rcN)
  IF (rhN<4000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: intervalo quente nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rhN)
  ppN:=OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)+OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)+OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmE,nEc,acw,dhw,lp,rhE,muE,bet,etH)+OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmC,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rhCo,muCo,bet,etC)
  IF (wn<=3*ppN/mhN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: monotonia nao garantida; requer procura de ramo propria.')
  reEN:=mlN*rmE/(nEc*acw)*dhw/muE
  reCN:=mlN*rmC/(nCc*acw)*dhw/muCo
  IF (reEN<2000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: intervalo cruza ramo de Martin; rever delimitacao XXX',reEN)
  IF (reCN<2000) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 12: intervalo cruza ramo de Martin; rever delimitacao XXX',reCN)
  iterN:=0
  REPEAT
    mmN:=(mlN+mhN)/2
    pmN:=mmN*wn-OTEC_Prede11(mmN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)-OTEC_Prede11(mmN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mmN,rmE,nEc,acw,dhw,lp,rhE,muE,bet,etH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mmN,rmC,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rhCo,muCo,bet,etC)
    IF (pmN>=Pn) THEN
      mhN:=mmN
    ELSE
      mlN:=mmN
    ENDIF
    iterN:=iterN+1
  UNTIL (iterN>=60)
  OTEC_mdisc12:=(mlN+mhN)/2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_Aev13(mN,ncN,rmN,awN,aaN,dwN,daN,muwN,kwN,cpwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,beN,rbN,cpN)
  $VarInfo mN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ncN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rmN Units='-'
  $VarInfo awN Units='-'
  $VarInfo aaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo dwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo daN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rtN Units='-'
  $VarInfo beN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rbN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Aev13 Units='-'
  IF (mN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: caudal invalido XXX',mN)
  IF (ncN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: contagem de canais invalida XXX',ncN)
  IF (rbN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: area especifica de vaporizacao invalida XXX',rbN)
  IF (cpN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: carga de pre-aquecimento invalida XXX',cpN)
  gwN:=mN*rmN/(ncN*awN)
  gaN:=mN/(ncN*aaN)
  hwN:=OTEC_h(gwN,dwN,muwN,kwN,cpwN,beN)
  haN:=OTEC_h(gaN,daN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,beN)
  uxN:=1/(1/hwN+rtN+1/haN)
  OTEC_Aev13:=mN*(rbN+cpN/uxN)
END

FUNCTION OTEC_mA13(aiN,mlN,mhN,ncN,rmN,awN,aaN,dwN,daN,muwN,kwN,cpwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,beN,rbN,cpN)
  $VarInfo aiN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mlN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mhN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ncN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rmN Units='-'
  $VarInfo awN Units='-'
  $VarInfo aaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo dwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo daN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rtN Units='-'
  $VarInfo beN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rbN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_mA13 Units='-'
  IF (aiN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: area instalada invalida XXX',aiN)
  IF (mlN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: limite inferior de caudal invalido XXX',mlN)
  IF (mhN<mlN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: limites de caudal invertidos.')
  alN:=OTEC_Aev13(mlN,ncN,rmN,awN,aaN,dwN,daN,muwN,kwN,cpwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,beN,rbN,cpN)
  ahN:=OTEC_Aev13(mhN,ncN,rmN,awN,aaN,dwN,daN,muwN,kwN,cpwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,beN,rbN,cpN)
  IF (alN>aiN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 13: evaporador ja insuficiente no ponto inicial.')
  IF (ahN<=aiN) THEN
    OTEC_mA13:=mhN
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  loN:=mlN
  hiN:=mhN
  itN:=0
  REPEAT
    mmN:=(loN+hiN)/2
    amN:=OTEC_Aev13(mmN,ncN,rmN,awN,aaN,dwN,daN,muwN,kwN,cpwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,beN,rbN,cpN)
    IF (amN>aiN) THEN
      hiN:=mmN
    ELSE
      loN:=mmN
    ENDIF
    itN:=itN+1
  UNTIL (itN>=60)
  OTEC_mA13:=(loN+hiN)/2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_mrob14(Pn,wn,mmax,rvC,rvH,nC,nH,aC,aH,dC,dH,lC,lH,rhC,rhH,muC,muH,epC,epH,etC,etH,kC,kH,nEc,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rmE,rmC,rhE,rhCo,muE,muCo,bet)

  $VarInfo kC Units='-'
  $VarInfo kH Units='-'
  $VarInfo nEc Units='-'
  $VarInfo nCc Units='-'
  $VarInfo acw Units='-'
  $VarInfo dhw Units='-'
  $VarInfo lp Units='-'
  $VarInfo rmE Units='-'
  $VarInfo rmC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhE Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhCo Units='-'
  $VarInfo muE Units='-'
  $VarInfo muCo Units='-'
  $VarInfo bet Units='-'
  $VarInfo Pn Units='-'
  $VarInfo wn Units='-'
  $VarInfo mmax Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rvH Units='-'
  $VarInfo nC Units='-'
  $VarInfo nH Units='-'
  $VarInfo aC Units='-'
  $VarInfo aH Units='-'
  $VarInfo dC Units='-'
  $VarInfo dH Units='-'
  $VarInfo lC Units='-'
  $VarInfo lH Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhC Units='-'
  $VarInfo rhH Units='-'
  $VarInfo muC Units='-'
  $VarInfo muH Units='-'
  $VarInfo epC Units='-'
  $VarInfo epH Units='-'
  $VarInfo etC Units='-'
  $VarInfo etH Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_mrob14 Units='-'
  IF (Pn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: meta de potencia invalida XXX',Pn)
  IF (wn<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: trabalho disponivel nao positivo XXX',wn)
  
  mlN:=MAX(Pn/wn,2000*muE*nEc*acw/(rmE*dhw),2000*muCo*nCc*acw/(rmC*dhw),4000*muC*nC*aC/(rhC*rvC*dC),4000*muH*nH*aH/(rhH*rvH*dH))
  mhN:=mmax
  IF (mhN<=mlN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: capacidade tubular insuficiente para a meta.')
  phN:=mhN*wn-OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)-OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmE,nEc,acw,dhw,lp,rhE,muE,bet,etH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmC,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rhCo,muCo,bet,etC)
  plN:=mlN*wn-OTEC_Prede11(mlN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)-OTEC_Prede11(mlN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mlN,rmE,nEc,acw,dhw,lp,rhE,muE,bet,etH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mlN,rmC,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rhCo,muCo,bet,etC)
  IF (plN>Pn) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 14: raiz abaixo do intervalo de correlacoes; requer outro ramo.')
  IF (phN<Pn) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: meta nao atingida no intervalo admissivel; rever ramos e tracado.')
  
  rcN:=rhC*(mlN*rvC/(nC*aC))*dC/muC
  rhN:=rhH*(mlN*rvH/(nH*aH))*dH/muH
  IF (rcN<3999.9999996) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: intervalo frio nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rcN)
  IF (rhN<3999.9999996) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: intervalo quente nao inteiramente turbulento XXX',rhN)
  ppN:=OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)+OTEC_Prede11(mhN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)+OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmE,nEc,acw,dhw,lp,rhE,muE,bet,etH)+OTEC_Pcan12(mhN,rmC,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rhCo,muCo,bet,etC)
  IF (wn<=3*ppN/mhN) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 10: monotonia nao garantida; requer procura de ramo propria.')
  reEN:=mlN*rmE/(nEc*acw)*dhw/muE
  reCN:=mlN*rmC/(nCc*acw)*dhw/muCo
  IF (reEN<1999.9999998) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 14: intervalo cruza ramo de Martin; rever delimitacao XXX',reEN)
  IF (reCN<1999.9999998) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 14: intervalo cruza ramo de Martin; rever delimitacao XXX',reCN)
  iterN:=0
  REPEAT
    mmN:=(mlN+mhN)/2
    pmN:=mmN*wn-OTEC_Prede11(mmN,rvC,nC,aC,dC,lC,rhC,muC,epC,etC,kC)-OTEC_Prede11(mmN,rvH,nH,aH,dH,lH,rhH,muH,epH,etH,kH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mmN,rmE,nEc,acw,dhw,lp,rhE,muE,bet,etH)-OTEC_Pcan12(mmN,rmC,nCc,acw,dhw,lp,rhCo,muCo,bet,etC)
    IF (pmN>=Pn) THEN
      mhN:=mmN
    ELSE
      mlN:=mmN
    ENDIF
    iterN:=iterN+1
  UNTIL (iterN>=60)
  OTEC_mrob14:=(mlN+mhN)/2
END

FUNCTION OTEC_hsw15(tN,flN)
  $VarInfo tN Units='-'
  $VarInfo flN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_hsw15 Units='-'
  IF (tN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: temperatura abaixo do ajuste TEOS XXX',tN)
  IF (tN>35) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: temperatura acima do ajuste TEOS XXX',tN)
  IF (flN<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: indice de fluido invalido XXX',flN)
  IF (flN>2) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: indice de fluido invalido XXX',flN)
  zN:=(tN-17.5)/17.5
  IF (flN=1) THEN
    pEvalN:=(7.41970403547747604E-13)
    pEvalN:=(5.59314445993826273E-13)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-5.61582736553764558E-05)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-8.15137316319471008E-04)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(4.90552933824537837E-03)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-7.68642925908707628E-03)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-4.60043926083735042E-03)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(9.30673438838717504E-02)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(6.99216724606545910E+01)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(6.98404645154742099E+01)+zN*pEvalN
    OTEC_hsw15:=pEvalN
  ELSE
    pEvalN:=(-1.67501079104124598E-14)
    pEvalN:=(3.16528921682553733E-13)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-5.61582718289392407E-05)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-8.15173084583956792E-04)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(4.90517631007896058E-03)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-7.68059268748103215E-03)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(-4.63077520058388994E-03)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(9.31620426575785060E-02)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(6.99199736817739108E+01)+zN*pEvalN
    pEvalN:=(6.98383008815891344E+01)+zN*pEvalN
    OTEC_hsw15:=pEvalN
  ENDIF
END

FUNCTION OTEC_cpsw15(tN,flN)

FUNCTION OTEC_Tsw15(hN,flN)
  $VarInfo hN Units='-'
  $VarInfo flN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_Tsw15 Units='-'
  
  IF (flN<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: indice invalido XXX',flN)
  IF (flN>2) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: indice invalido XXX',flN)
  IF (flN=1) THEN
    IF (hN<OTEC_hsw15(0,1)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: entalpia abaixo do dominio XXX',hN)
    IF (hN>OTEC_hsw15(35,1)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: entalpia acima do dominio XXX',hN)
    zInv15:=(hN-(6.99250302927831768E+01))/(6.99219213924593959E+01)
    pInv15:=(3.44783131580283363E-09)
    pInv15:=(3.13373979621792915E-09)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-1.53475056799835802E-07)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(6.83947076028171906E-07)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-1.24022316958560034E-06)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.19788000683255497E-05)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(2.16536153458983776E-04)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-1.24129953859037170E-03)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.90840713622557421E-03)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.22266838973707779E-03)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-2.32886463308598878E-02)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.75000059652682545E+01)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.75211650932915717E+01)+zInv15*pInv15
    OTEC_Tsw15:=pInv15
  ELSE
    IF (hN<OTEC_hsw15(0,2)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: entalpia abaixo do dominio XXX',hN)
    IF (hN>OTEC_hsw15(35,2)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: entalpia acima do dominio XXX',hN)
    zInv15:=(hN-(6.99229671584749184E+01))/(6.99201919246118138E+01)
    pInv15:=(3.44294456840412871E-09)
    pInv15:=(3.14737509659062790E-09)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-1.53498809790070629E-07)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(6.83800720485891437E-07)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-1.23851934157446563E-06)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.19709651407923548E-05)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(2.16566220627061908E-04)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-1.24123746615138189E-03)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.90692153966189677E-03)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.23042082355305418E-03)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(-2.33128602379704501E-02)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.74999981587297100E+01)+zInv15*pInv15
    pInv15:=(1.75211907610528996E+01)+zInv15*pInv15
    OTEC_Tsw15:=pInv15
  ENDIF
END

FUNCTION OTEC_LM15(aN,bN)
  $VarInfo aN Units='-'
  $VarInfo bN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_LM15 Units='-'
  IF (aN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: diferenca terminal nao positiva XXX',aN)
  IF (bN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: diferenca terminal nao positiva XXX',bN)
  IF (ABS(aN-bN)<0.00000001) THEN
    OTEC_LM15:=(aN+bN)/2
  ELSE
    OTEC_LM15:=(aN-bN)/LN(aN/bN)
  ENDIF
END

{OTEC_ev15/co15: tip=1 uso max(area,pinch); 2 area m2; 3 minDT K;
 4 U_pre kW/(m2 K) no evaporador ou Tout C no condensador.
 1E30 indica tentativa inviavel; nao e um resultado fisico.}

FUNCTION OTEC_ev15(tipN,mN,tiN,mwN,arN,ubN,ncN,acN,aaN,dhN,daN,beN,spN,kwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,qeN,qbN,qpN,teN,piN,ta0N,ta1N,ta2N,ta3N,ta4N,ta5N,ta6N,ta7N,ta8N,ta9N,ta10N)
  $VarInfo tipN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mN Units='-'
  $VarInfo tiN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo arN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ubN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ncN Units='-'
  $VarInfo acN Units='-'
  $VarInfo aaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo dhN Units='-'
  $VarInfo daN Units='-'
  $VarInfo beN Units='-'
  $VarInfo spN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rtN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qeN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qbN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qpN Units='-'
  $VarInfo teN Units='-'
  $VarInfo piN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta0N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta1N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta2N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta3N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta4N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta5N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta6N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta7N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta8N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta9N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta10N Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_ev15 Units='-'
  IF (mN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: caudal de amonia negativo XXX',mN)
  IF (mN=0) THEN
    OTEC_ev15:=0
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  hiN:=OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)
  hoN:=hiN-mN*qeN/mwN
  hbN:=hiN-mN*qbN/mwN
  
  IF (tiN<=teN) THEN
    OTEC_ev15:=1E30
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  IF (hoN<=OTEC_hsw15(ta0N,1)) THEN
    OTEC_ev15:=1E30
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  IF (hbN<=OTEC_hsw15(teN,1)) THEN
    OTEC_ev15:=1E30
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  toN:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN,1)
  tbN:=OTEC_Tsw15(hbN,1)
  dt0N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+0*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta0N
  dt1N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+1*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta1N
  dt2N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+2*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta2N
  dt3N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+3*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta3N
  dt4N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+4*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta4N
  dt5N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+5*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta5N
  dt6N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+6*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta6N
  dt7N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+7*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta7N
  dt8N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+8*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta8N
  dt9N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+9*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta9N
  dt10N:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN+10*mN*qpN/(10*mwN),1)-ta10N
  dmN:=MIN(tiN-teN,tbN-teN,dt0N,dt1N,dt2N,dt3N,dt4N,dt5N,dt6N,dt7N,dt8N,dt9N,dt10N)
  IF (dmN<=0) THEN
    OTEC_ev15:=1E30
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  tmN:=(tiN+toN)/2
  muwN:=OTEC_muSW_tubo(tmN,spN)
  cpwN:=OTEC_cpsw15(tmN,1)
  hwN:=OTEC_h(mwN/(ncN*acN),dhN,muwN,kwN,cpwN,beN)
  haN:=OTEC_h(mN/(ncN*aaN),daN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,beN)
  upN:=1/(1/hwN+rtN+1/haN)/1000
  crN:=(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt0N,dt1N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt1N,dt2N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt2N,dt3N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt3N,dt4N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt4N,dt5N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt5N,dt6N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt6N,dt7N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt7N,dt8N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt8N,dt9N)+(qpN/10)/OTEC_LM15(dt9N,dt10N)
  areaN:=mN*(crN/upN+qbN/(ubN*OTEC_LM15(tiN-teN,tbN-teN)))
  OTEC_ev15:=MAX(areaN/arN,piN/dmN)
  IF (tipN=2) THEN OTEC_ev15:=areaN
  IF (tipN=3) THEN OTEC_ev15:=dmN
  IF (tipN=4) THEN OTEC_ev15:=upN
END

FUNCTION OTEC_co15(tipN,mN,tiN,mwN,arN,ucN,qcN,tcN,piN)
  $VarInfo tipN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mN Units='-'
  $VarInfo tiN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo arN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ucN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo tcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo piN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_co15 Units='-'
  IF (mN<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: caudal de amonia negativo XXX',mN)
  IF (mN=0) THEN
    OTEC_co15:=0
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  hiN:=OTEC_hsw15(tiN,2)
  hoN:=hiN+mN*qcN/mwN
  IF (tiN>=tcN) THEN
    OTEC_co15:=1E30
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  IF (hoN>=OTEC_hsw15(tcN,2)) THEN
    OTEC_co15:=1E30
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  toN:=OTEC_Tsw15(hoN,2)
  dmN:=MIN(tcN-tiN,tcN-toN)
  areaN:=mN*qcN/(ucN*OTEC_LM15(tcN-tiN,tcN-toN))
  OTEC_co15:=MAX(areaN/arN,piN/dmN)
  IF (tipN=2) THEN OTEC_co15:=areaN
  IF (tipN=3) THEN OTEC_co15:=dmN
  IF (tipN=4) THEN OTEC_co15:=toN
END

FUNCTION OTEC_mEv15(mxN,tiN,mwN,arN,ubN,ncN,acN,aaN,dhN,daN,beN,spN,kwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,qeN,qbN,qpN,teN,piN,ta0N,ta1N,ta2N,ta3N,ta4N,ta5N,ta6N,ta7N,ta8N,ta9N,ta10N)
  $VarInfo mxN Units='-'
  $VarInfo tiN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo arN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ubN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ncN Units='-'
  $VarInfo acN Units='-'
  $VarInfo aaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo dhN Units='-'
  $VarInfo daN Units='-'
  $VarInfo beN Units='-'
  $VarInfo spN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo muaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo kaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo cpaN Units='-'
  $VarInfo rtN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qeN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qbN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qpN Units='-'
  $VarInfo teN Units='-'
  $VarInfo piN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta0N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta1N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta2N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta3N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta4N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta5N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta6N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta7N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta8N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta9N Units='-'
  $VarInfo ta10N Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_mEv15 Units='-'
  IF (mxN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: caudal maximo invalido XXX',mxN)
  IF (tiN<=teN+piN) THEN
    OTEC_mEv15:=0
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  eLim015:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta0N+piN,1))/(qeN-0*qpN/10)
  eLim115:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta1N+piN,1))/(qeN-1*qpN/10)
  eLim215:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta2N+piN,1))/(qeN-2*qpN/10)
  eLim315:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta3N+piN,1))/(qeN-3*qpN/10)
  eLim415:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta4N+piN,1))/(qeN-4*qpN/10)
  eLim515:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta5N+piN,1))/(qeN-5*qpN/10)
  eLim615:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta6N+piN,1))/(qeN-6*qpN/10)
  eLim715:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta7N+piN,1))/(qeN-7*qpN/10)
  eLim815:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta8N+piN,1))/(qeN-8*qpN/10)
  eLim915:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta9N+piN,1))/(qeN-9*qpN/10)
  eLim1015:=mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tiN,1)-OTEC_hsw15(ta10N+piN,1))/(qeN-10*qpN/10)
  evB15:=MAX(0,MIN(mxN,eLim015,eLim115,eLim215,eLim315,eLim415,eLim515,eLim615,eLim715,eLim815,eLim915,eLim1015))
  IF (evB15<=0) THEN
    OTEC_mEv15:=0
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  evB15Area:=OTEC_ev15(2,evB15,tiN,mwN,arN,ubN,ncN,acN,aaN,dhN,daN,beN,spN,kwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,qeN,qbN,qpN,teN,piN,ta0N,ta1N,ta2N,ta3N,ta4N,ta5N,ta6N,ta7N,ta8N,ta9N,ta10N)
  IF (evB15Area<=arN*(1+0.000000001)) THEN
    OTEC_mEv15:=evB15
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  evB15Lo:=0
  evB15Hi:=evB15
  evB15Iter:=0
  REPEAT
    evB15Mid:=(evB15Lo+evB15Hi)/2
    evB15Area:=OTEC_ev15(2,evB15Mid,tiN,mwN,arN,ubN,ncN,acN,aaN,dhN,daN,beN,spN,kwN,muaN,kaN,cpaN,rtN,qeN,qbN,qpN,teN,piN,ta0N,ta1N,ta2N,ta3N,ta4N,ta5N,ta6N,ta7N,ta8N,ta9N,ta10N)
    IF (evB15Area>arN) THEN
      evB15Hi:=evB15Mid
    ELSE
      evB15Lo:=evB15Mid
    ENDIF
    evB15Iter:=evB15Iter+1
  UNTIL (evB15Iter>=50)
  OTEC_mEv15:=evB15Lo
END

FUNCTION OTEC_mCo15(mxN,tiN,mwN,arN,ucN,qcN,tcN,piN)
  $VarInfo mxN Units='-'
  $VarInfo tiN Units='-'
  $VarInfo mwN Units='-'
  $VarInfo arN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ucN Units='-'
  $VarInfo qcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo tcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo piN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_mCo15 Units='-'
  IF (mxN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: caudal maximo invalido XXX',mxN)
  IF (tiN>=tcN-piN) THEN
    OTEC_mCo15:=0
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  coB15:=MAX(0,MIN(mxN,mwN*(OTEC_hsw15(tcN-piN,2)-OTEC_hsw15(tiN,2))/qcN))
  IF (coB15<=0) THEN
    OTEC_mCo15:=0
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  coB15Area:=OTEC_co15(2,coB15,tiN,mwN,arN,ucN,qcN,tcN,piN)
  IF (coB15Area<=arN*(1+0.000000001)) THEN
    OTEC_mCo15:=coB15
    RETURN
  ENDIF
  coB15Lo:=0
  coB15Hi:=coB15
  coB15Iter:=0
  REPEAT
    coB15Mid:=(coB15Lo+coB15Hi)/2
    coB15Area:=OTEC_co15(2,coB15Mid,tiN,mwN,arN,ucN,qcN,tcN,piN)
    IF (coB15Area>arN) THEN
      coB15Hi:=coB15Mid
    ELSE
      coB15Lo:=coB15Mid
    ENDIF
    coB15Iter:=coB15Iter+1
  UNTIL (coB15Iter>=50)
  OTEC_mCo15:=coB15Lo
END

FUNCTION OTEC_val15(aeV,acV,aeI,acI,deV,dcV,peV,pcV,mV,mMaxV)
  $VarInfo aeV Units='-'
  $VarInfo acV Units='-'
  $VarInfo aeI Units='-'
  $VarInfo acI Units='-'
  $VarInfo deV Units='-'
  $VarInfo dcV Units='-'
  $VarInfo peV Units='-'
  $VarInfo pcV Units='-'
  $VarInfo mV Units='-'
  $VarInfo mMaxV Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_val15 Units='-'
  IF (aeV>aeI*(1+0.0000001)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: area requerida do evaporador excedida XXX',aeV)
  IF (acV>acI*(1+0.0000001)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: area requerida do condensador excedida XXX',acV)
  IF (deV+0.0000001<peV) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: pinch do evaporador violado XXX',deV)
  IF (dcV+0.0000001<pcV) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: pinch do condensador violado XXX',dcV)
  IF (mV<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: caudal negativo XXX',mV)
  IF (mV>mMaxV*(1+0.0000001)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 15: limite de carga excedido XXX',mV)
  OTEC_val15:=1
END

FUNCTION OTEC_ssw16(tS16,fS16)
  $VarInfo tS16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo fS16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_ssw16 Units='-'
  IF (tS16<0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: temperatura abaixo do dominio TEOS XXX',tS16)
  IF (tS16>35) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: temperatura acima do dominio TEOS XXX',tS16)
  IF (fS16<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: indice de fluido invalido XXX',fS16)
  IF (fS16>2) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: indice de fluido invalido XXX',fS16)
  zS16:=(tS16-17.5)/17.5
  IF (fS16=1) THEN
    pS16:=(8.74496875988303040E-14)
    pS16:=(-1.44606133573336682E-14)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-2.31377425595102477E-13)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(3.35253951610449245E-14)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.26819435713206134E-13)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-2.51304661912744985E-14)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-3.74388487775001628E-06)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(1.87215631208063352E-05)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-3.82780701331517835E-05)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.62026044164219267E-04)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-6.92214077117363127E-03)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.40570006745758430E-01)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.47868028721406924E-01)+zS16*pS16
    OTEC_ssw16:=pS16
  ELSE
    pS16:=(4.60996915777436582E-14)
    pS16:=(-1.47037318085185430E-15)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-1.20484766018139707E-13)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.09328124062926817E-15)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(1.16135290246214268E-13)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(6.33929689058251723E-16)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-3.74388482961236774E-06)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(1.87191104020206366E-05)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-3.82523663161463538E-05)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.61901530276199888E-04)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(-6.92163899788901440E-03)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.40564161987868591E-01)+zS16*pS16
    pS16:=(2.47855822343631099E-01)+zS16*pS16
    OTEC_ssw16:=pS16
  ENDIF
END

FUNCTION OTEC_valEx16(dMin16,eIn16,eUse16,eMin16,rEx16,rEl16,saErr16)
  $VarInfo dMin16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo eIn16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo eUse16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo eMin16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo rEx16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo rEl16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo saErr16 Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_valEx16 Units='-'
  IF (dMin16<(-0.001)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: destruicao de exergia negativa XXX',dMin16)
  IF (eIn16<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: exergia termica de entrada nao positiva XXX',eIn16)
  IF (eUse16<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: deplecao de exergia nao positiva XXX',eUse16)
  IF (eMin16<(-0.000001)) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: exergia especifica fisica negativa XXX',eMin16)
  IF (ABS(rEx16)>0.00000001) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: residuo relativo exergia excedido XXX',rEx16)
  IF (ABS(rEl16)>0.00000001) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: residuo relativo electrico excedido XXX',rEl16)
  IF (saErr16>0.00000001) THEN CALL ERROR('Parte 16: salinidades alteradas; regenerar ajustes XXX',saErr16)
  OTEC_valEx16:=1
END

FUNCTION OTEC_escolha17B(a1N,p1N,a2N,p2N,a3N,p3N,a4N,p4N)
  $VarInfo a1N Units='-'
  $VarInfo p1N Units='-'
  $VarInfo a2N Units='-'
  $VarInfo p2N Units='-'
  $VarInfo a3N Units='-'
  $VarInfo p3N Units='-'
  $VarInfo a4N Units='-'
  $VarInfo p4N Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_escolha17B Units='-'
  idxN:=0
  melhorN:=0
  IF (a1N=1) THEN
    IF (idxN=0) THEN
      idxN:=1
      melhorN:=p1N
    ELSE
      IF (p1N<melhorN-0.001) THEN
        idxN:=1
        melhorN:=p1N
      ENDIF
    ENDIF
  ENDIF
  IF (a2N=1) THEN
    IF (idxN=0) THEN
      idxN:=2
      melhorN:=p2N
    ELSE
      IF (p2N<melhorN-0.001) THEN
        idxN:=2
        melhorN:=p2N
      ENDIF
    ENDIF
  ENDIF
  IF (a3N=1) THEN
    IF (idxN=0) THEN
      idxN:=3
      melhorN:=p3N
    ELSE
      IF (p3N<melhorN-0.001) THEN
        idxN:=3
        melhorN:=p3N
      ENDIF
    ENDIF
  ENDIF
  IF (a4N=1) THEN
    IF (idxN=0) THEN
      idxN:=4
      melhorN:=p4N
    ELSE
      IF (p4N<melhorN-0.001) THEN
        idxN:=4
        melhorN:=p4N
      ENDIF
    ENDIF
  ENDIF
  OTEC_escolha17B:=idxN
END




{Ciclo NH3 sequencial; entradas/saidas numericas nas unidades SI do main.
 Indices 1..25 documentados pelas chamadas. Estados nao sao tentativas
 independentes do solver: todos pertencem ao mesmo par Te/Tc.}
FUNCTION OTEC_cy17(qualN,teN,tcN,etN,ebN,jN)
  $VarInfo qualN Units='-'
  $VarInfo teN Units='-'
  $VarInfo tcN Units='-'
  $VarInfo etN Units='-'
  $VarInfo ebN Units='-'
  $VarInfo jN Units='-'
  $VarInfo OTEC_cy17 Units='-'
  $VarInfo teX Units='C'
  $VarInfo tcX Units='C'
  $VarInfo pEX Units='kPa'
  $VarInfo pCX Units='kPa'
  $VarInfo hAX Units='kJ/kg'
  $VarInfo hBX Units='kJ/kg'
  $VarInfo hCX Units='kJ/kg'
  $VarInfo hDX Units='kJ/kg'
  $VarInfo hBSX Units='kJ/kg'
  $VarInfo hDSX Units='kJ/kg'
  $VarInfo hLX Units='kJ/kg'
  $VarInfo sAX Units='kJ/kg-K'
  $VarInfo sCX Units='kJ/kg-K'
  $VarInfo tBX Units='C'
  $VarInfo tMX Units='C'
  $VarInfo hJX Units='kJ/kg'
  IF (qualN<1) THEN CALL ERROR('Ciclo 17: indice de retorno invalido XXX',qualN)
  IF (qualN>25) THEN CALL ERROR('Ciclo 17: indice de retorno invalido XXX',qualN)
  IF (tcN<=0) THEN CALL ERROR('Ciclo 17: temperatura condensacao invalida XXX',tcN)
  IF (teN<=tcN) THEN CALL ERROR('Ciclo 17: evaporacao deve superar condensacao XXX',teN)
  teX:=0 [C]+teN*1 [DELTAC]
  tcX:=0 [C]+tcN*1 [DELTAC]
  pEX:=Pressure(Ammonia,T=teX,x=1)
  pCX:=Pressure(Ammonia,T=tcX,x=0)
  hAX:=Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=pCX,x=0)
  sAX:=Entropy(Ammonia,P=pCX,x=0)
  hBSX:=Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=pEX,s=sAX)
  hBX:=hAX+(hBSX-hAX)/ebN
  hCX:=Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=pEX,x=1)
  sCX:=Entropy(Ammonia,P=pEX,x=1)
  hDSX:=Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=pCX,s=sCX)
  hDX:=hCX-etN*(hCX-hDSX)
  hLX:=Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=pEX,x=0)
  tBX:=Temperature(Ammonia,P=pEX,h=hBX)
  tMX:=(tBX+teX)/2
  valorN:=0
  IF (qualN=1) THEN valorN:=pEX/(1 [kPa])
  IF (qualN=2) THEN valorN:=pCX/(1 [kPa])
  IF (qualN=3) THEN valorN:=hAX/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=4) THEN valorN:=hBX/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=5) THEN valorN:=hCX/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=6) THEN valorN:=hDX/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=7) THEN valorN:=sAX/(1 [kJ/kg-K])
  IF (qualN=8) THEN valorN:=sCX/(1 [kJ/kg-K])
  IF (qualN=9) THEN valorN:=hBSX/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=10) THEN valorN:=hDSX/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=11) THEN valorN:=hLX/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=12) THEN valorN:=(tBX-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  IF (qualN=13) THEN valorN:=(hCX-hBX)/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=14) THEN valorN:=(hLX-hBX)/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=15) THEN valorN:=(hCX-hLX)/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=16) THEN valorN:=(hDX-hAX)/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=17) THEN valorN:=(hCX-hDX)/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=18) THEN valorN:=(hBX-hAX)/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=19) THEN
    hJX:=hBX+(jN-1)*(hLX-hBX)/10
    valorN:=(Temperature(Ammonia,P=pEX,h=hJX)-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  ENDIF
  IF (qualN=20) THEN valorN:=(tMX-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  IF (qualN=21) THEN valorN:=Viscosity(Ammonia,T=tMX,P=pEX)/(1 [Pa-s])
  IF (qualN=22) THEN valorN:=Conductivity(Ammonia,T=tMX,P=pEX)/(1 [W/m-K])
  IF (qualN=23) THEN valorN:=Cp(Ammonia,T=tMX,P=pEX)*Convert(kJ,J)/(1 [J/kg-K])
  IF (qualN=24) THEN valorN:=(hBX+(jN-1)*(hLX-hBX)/10)/(1 [kJ/kg])
  IF (qualN=25) THEN valorN:=Quality(Ammonia,P=pCX,h=hDX)
  OTEC_cy17:=valorN
END

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 1 - ENTRADAS DO CASO MEDIO PRELIMINAR}

{1. DADOS OCEANOGRAFICOS}

theta_aq_med = 27.87 [C]
theta_af_med = 5.89 [C]
SP_aq = 34.822
SP_af = 34.835

z_aq_dados = 0.49 [m]
z_af_dados = 1062.44 [m]
lat_SP = -14.333 [deg]
lon_SP = 40.917 [deg]

{2. TEMPERATURAS PRESCRITAS NO ENSAIO PRELIMINAR}

T_aq_in = theta_aq_med
T_af_in = theta_af_med
DT_recurso = T_aq_in - T_af_in

{3. META E REFERENCIA AMBIENTAL}

P_liq_meta = 10000 [kW]
P_amb = 101.325 [kPa]
T0 = 25 [C]
P0 = P_amb

{4. HIPOTESES TERMICAS DO MODELO ORIGINAL}

DT_aq_proj = 4 [DELTAC]
DT_af_proj = 4 [DELTAC]
DTpinch_ev = 1.2 [DELTAC]
DTpinch_cond = 1.1 [DELTAC]

U_pre_ref_antigo = 3.5 [kW/m^2-K]
U_boil_ev = 4 [kW/m^2-K]
U_phase_cond = 4 [kW/m^2-K]

{5. RENDIMENTOS: HIPOTESES DE PRE-DIMENSIONAMENTO}

eta_t = 0.85
eta_b_am = 0.85
eta_ger = 0.95
eta_b_aq = 0.85
eta_b_af = 0.85
eta_m_aq = 0.95
eta_m_af = 0.95
eta_m_am = 0.95

{6. REFERENCIAS INICIAIS PARA O PRE-DIMENSIONAMENTO}

v_canal_ref = 1 [m/s]
v_tubo_max = 2 [m/s]

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 2 - CICLO DE AMONIA}

{8. UNIDADES EXPLICITAS}
$VarInfo DT_recurso Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo T_aq_in Units='C'
$VarInfo T_af_in Units='C'
$VarInfo P0 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo T_aq_out Units='C'
$VarInfo T_af_out Units='C'
$VarInfo T_ev Units='C'
$VarInfo T_cond Units='C'
$VarInfo DT_ciclo Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo R_energia_esp Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo R_energia_rel Units='-'

{9. FECHO TERMICO INICIAL}

T_aq_out = T_aq_in - DT_aq_proj
T_af_out = T_af_in + DT_af_proj
T_ev = T_aq_out - DTpinch_ev
T_cond = T_af_out + DTpinch_cond
DT_ciclo = T_ev - T_cond

{10. PRESSOES ABSOLUTAS DE SATURACAO}
P_ev = Pressure(Ammonia,T=T_ev,x=1)
P_cond = Pressure(Ammonia,T=T_cond,x=0)

{11. ESTADO 1: LIQUIDO SATURADO, SAIDA DO CONDENSADOR}
P[1] = P_cond
T[1] = T_cond
h[1] = Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=P[1],x=0)
s[1] = Entropy(Ammonia,P=P[1],x=0)

{12. ESTADO 2: SAIDA REAL DA BOMBA DE AMONIA}
P[2] = P_ev
h_2s = Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=P[2],s=s[1])
h[2] = h[1] + (h_2s-h[1])/eta_b_am
T[2] = Temperature(Ammonia,P=P[2],h=h[2])
s[2] = Entropy(Ammonia,P=P[2],h=h[2])

{13. ESTADO 3: VAPOR SATURADO, ENTRADA DA TURBINA}
P[3] = P_ev
T[3] = T_ev
h[3] = Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=P[3],x=1)
s[3] = Entropy(Ammonia,P=P[3],x=1)

{14. ESTADO 4: SAIDA REAL DA TURBINA}
P[4] = P_cond
h_4s = Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=P[4],s=s[3])
h[4] = h[3] - eta_t*(h[3]-h_4s)
T[4] = Temperature(Ammonia,P=P[4],h=h[4])
s[4] = Entropy(Ammonia,P=P[4],h=h[4])
x_4 = Quality(Ammonia,P=P[4],h=h[4])

{15. BALANCOS POR kg DE AMONIA}
q_ev = h[3] - h[2]
q_cond = h[4] - h[1]
w_t = h[3] - h[4]
w_b_am = h[2] - h[1]
w_eixo_ciclo = w_t - w_b_am
eta_term_ciclo = w_eixo_ciclo/q_ev

w_ger = eta_ger*w_t
w_b_am_el = w_b_am/eta_m_am

{16. VERIFICACAO DA CONSERVACAO DE ENERGIA}
R_energia_esp = q_ev - q_cond - w_t + w_b_am
R_energia_rel = ABS(R_energia_esp)/ABS(q_ev)

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 3 - AGUA DO MAR E CAUDAIS RELATIVOS}

{17. UNIDADES}
$VarInfo dh_aq Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo dh_af Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo cp_aq_ef Units='kJ/kg-K'
$VarInfo cp_af_ef Units='kJ/kg-K'
$VarInfo r_m_aq Units='-'
$VarInfo r_m_af Units='-'
$VarInfo r_V_aq Units='m^3/kg'
$VarInfo r_V_af Units='m^3/kg'
$VarInfo R_agua_aq Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo R_agua_af Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo R_tabela_max Units='-'

{18. PROPRIEDADES TABULADAS PARA ESTE CASO}

SA_aq = 34.986434046 [g/kg]
h_aq_in = 111.305146312 [kJ/kg]
h_aq_out = 95.303956936 [kJ/kg]
rho_aq_in = 1022.304313236 [kg/m^3]
rho_aq_out = 1023.548208156 [kg/m^3]
rho_aq_med = 1022.941656314 [kg/m^3]

SA_af = 35.005680627 [g/kg]
h_af_in = 23.491564285 [kJ/kg]
h_af_out = 39.450742431 [kJ/kg]
rho_af_in = 1027.441914322 [kg/m^3]
rho_af_out = 1026.848972305 [kg/m^3]
rho_af_med = 1027.166956680 [kg/m^3]

{19. CONTROLO DA VALIDADE DA TABELA}

R_tab[1] = ABS((T_aq_in-27.87 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]))
R_tab[2] = ABS((T_aq_out-23.87 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]))
R_tab[3] = ABS((T_af_in-5.89 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]))
R_tab[4] = ABS((T_af_out-9.89 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]))
R_tab[5] = ABS(SP_aq-34.822)
R_tab[6] = ABS(SP_af-34.835)
R_tab[7] = ABS((P_amb-101.325 [kPa])/(1 [kPa]))
R_tab[8] = ABS((lat_SP+14.333 [deg])/(1 [deg]))
R_tab[9] = ABS((lon_SP-40.917 [deg])/(1 [deg]))
R_tab[10] = ABS((z_aq_dados-0.49 [m])/(1 [m]))
R_tab[11] = ABS((z_af_dados-1062.44 [m])/(1 [m]))

R_tabela_max = MAX(R_tab[1],R_tab[2],R_tab[3],R_tab[4],R_tab[5],R_tab[6],R_tab[7],R_tab[8],R_tab[9],R_tab[10],R_tab[11])

{20. VARIACOES DE ENTALPIA DA AGUA}

dh_aq = h_aq_in-h_aq_out
dh_af = h_af_out-h_af_in

cp_aq_ef = dh_aq/DT_aq_proj
cp_af_ef = dh_af/DT_af_proj

{21. CAUDAIS MASSICOS RELATIVOS}

r_m_aq = q_ev/dh_aq
r_m_af = q_cond/dh_af

{22. CAUDAIS VOLUMETRICOS RELATIVOS NAS ENTRADAS}

r_V_aq = r_m_aq/rho_aq_in
r_V_af = r_m_af/rho_af_in

{23. RESIDUOS DOS BALANCOS POR kg DE AMONIA}

R_agua_aq = q_ev-r_m_aq*dh_aq
R_agua_af = q_cond-r_m_af*dh_af

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 4 - AREAS DOS PERMUTADORES}

{25. UNIDADES}
$VarInfo q_pre_ev Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo q_boil_ev Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo DT_boil_1 Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_boil_2 Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_cond_1 Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_cond_2 Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_boil Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_cond Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTmin_ev Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[1] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[2] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[3] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[4] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[5] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[6] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[7] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[8] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[9] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[10] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DT_pre[11] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[1] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[2] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[3] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[4] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[5] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[6] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[7] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[8] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[9] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo DTlm_pre[10] Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[1] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[2] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[3] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[4] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[5] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[6] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[7] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[8] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[9] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[10] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_aq_pre[11] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[1] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[2] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[3] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[4] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[5] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[6] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[7] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[8] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[9] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[10] Units='C'
$VarInfo T_am_pre[11] Units='C'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[1] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[2] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[3] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[4] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[5] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[6] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[7] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[8] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[9] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_pre_seg[10] Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[1] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[2] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[3] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[4] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[5] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[6] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[7] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[8] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[9] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U_seg[10] Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo C_pre_U Units='J/kg-K'
$VarInfo beta_M_num Units='-'
$VarInfo U_pre_num Units='-'
$VarInfo DTmin_cond Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo Margem_ev Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo Margem_cond Units='DELTAC'
$VarInfo rA_pre_ev Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_boil_ev Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_ev Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_cond Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo R_zonas_ev Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo R_T_cond Units='DELTAC'

{26. SEPARACAO DAS CARGAS DO EVAPORADOR}
h_ev_liq = Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=P_ev,x=0)
q_pre_ev = h_ev_liq-h[2]
q_boil_ev = h[3]-h_ev_liq
R_zonas_ev = q_ev-q_pre_ev-q_boil_ev

{27. INTERPOLACAO LOCAL DA ENTALPIA DA AGUA QUENTE}

C_h0 = 103.30349851348 [kJ/kg]
C_h1 = 8.0006095151436 [kJ/kg]
C_h2 = 0.001053073267445 [kJ/kg]
C_h3 = -0.000014832357263899 [kJ/kg]

{28. PRE-AQUECIMENTO EM DEZ SEGMENTOS}

N_pre = 10

Duplicate i=1,N_pre+1
  T_aq_pre[i] = 25.87 [C]+2 [DELTAC]*u_pre[i]
  h_aq_out+(i-1)*q_pre_ev/(N_pre*r_m_aq) = C_h0+C_h1*u_pre[i]+C_h2*u_pre[i]^2+C_h3*u_pre[i]^3
  T_am_pre[i] = Temperature(Ammonia,P=P_ev,h=h[2]+(i-1)*q_pre_ev/N_pre)
  DT_pre[i] = T_aq_pre[i]-T_am_pre[i]
End

Duplicate j=1,N_pre
  DTlm_pre[j] = (DT_pre[j]-DT_pre[j+1])/LN(DT_pre[j]/DT_pre[j+1])
  rA_pre_seg[j] = (q_pre_ev/N_pre)/(U_pre_ev*DTlm_pre[j])
End

rA_pre_ev = SUM(rA_pre_seg[1..N_pre])
T_aq_int = T_aq_pre[N_pre+1]

{29. ZONA DE VAPORIZACAO}
DT_boil_1 = T_aq_in-T_ev
DT_boil_2 = T_aq_int-T_ev
DTlm_boil = (DT_boil_1-DT_boil_2)/LN(DT_boil_1/DT_boil_2)
rA_boil_ev = q_boil_ev/(U_boil_ev*DTlm_boil)
rA_ev = rA_pre_ev+rA_boil_ev

{30. CONDENSADOR: ENTRADA BIFASICA NESTE CASO}

h_cond_vap = Enthalpy(Ammonia,P=P_cond,x=1)
q_dessob_cond = MAX(0 [kJ/kg],h[4]-h_cond_vap)
R_T_cond = ABS(T[4]-T_cond)

DT_cond_1 = T_cond-T_af_in
DT_cond_2 = T_cond-T_af_out
DTlm_cond = (DT_cond_1-DT_cond_2)/LN(DT_cond_1/DT_cond_2)
rA_cond = q_cond/(U_phase_cond*DTlm_cond)

{31. APROXIMACOES MINIMAS E MARGENS}

DTmin_ev = MIN(DT_pre[1..N_pre+1],DT_boil_1,DT_boil_2)
DTmin_cond = MIN(DT_cond_1,DT_cond_2)
Margem_ev = DTmin_ev-DTpinch_ev
Margem_cond = DTmin_cond-DTpinch_cond

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 5 - GEOMETRIA CONTINUA DE REFERENCIA}

{32. UNIDADES}
$VarInfo rNc_ev_area Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rNc_cond_area Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rNc_ev_vel Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rNc_cond_vel Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rNc_ev_eq Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rNc_cond_eq Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rNp_ev_eq Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rNp_cond_eq Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_ev_geom Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_cond_geom Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo G_aq_canal Units='kg/m^2-s'
$VarInfo G_af_canal Units='kg/m^2-s'
$VarInfo G_am_ev Units='kg/m^2-s'
$VarInfo G_am_cond Units='kg/m^2-s'
$VarInfo v_aq_canal_max Units='m/s'
$VarInfo v_af_canal_max Units='m/s'
$VarInfo v_am_ev_out Units='m/s'
$VarInfo v_am_cond_in Units='m/s'
$VarInfo Margem_v_aq Units='m/s'
$VarInfo Margem_v_af Units='m/s'

{33. DIMENSOES INICIAIS DO MODELO ORIGINAL}

L_placa = 1.468 [m]
B_placa = 0.605 [m]
t_placa = 0.0006 [m]
b_ag = 0.005 [m]
b_am = 0.005 [m]
beta_PHE = 60 [deg]

Phi_placa = 1.20
A_placa_proj = L_placa*B_placa
A_placa_ef = Phi_placa*A_placa_proj
A_canal_ag = B_placa*b_ag
A_canal_am = B_placa*b_am

Dh_ag = 2*b_ag/Phi_placa
Dh_am = 2*b_am/Phi_placa
Dh_rect_ag = 2*B_placa*b_ag/(B_placa+b_ag)

{34. DENSIDADES PARA A MAIOR VELOCIDADE DA AGUA}

rho_aq_min = MIN(rho_aq_in,rho_aq_out)
rho_af_min = MIN(rho_af_in,rho_af_out)

{35. NECESSIDADE CONTINUA DE CANAIS}

rNc_ev_area = rA_ev/(2*A_placa_ef)
rNc_cond_area = rA_cond/(2*A_placa_ef)

rNc_ev_vel = r_m_aq/(rho_aq_min*v_canal_ref*A_canal_ag)
rNc_cond_vel = r_m_af/(rho_af_min*v_canal_ref*A_canal_ag)

rNc_ev_eq = MAX(rNc_ev_area,rNc_ev_vel)
rNc_cond_eq = MAX(rNc_cond_area,rNc_cond_vel)

{36. PLACAS E AREAS EQUIVALENTES}

rNp_ev_eq = 2*rNc_ev_eq
rNp_cond_eq = 2*rNc_cond_eq
rA_ev_geom = rNp_ev_eq*A_placa_ef
rA_cond_geom = rNp_cond_eq*A_placa_ef

F_area_ev = rA_ev_geom/rA_ev
F_area_cond = rA_cond_geom/rA_cond

{37. FLUXOS MASSICOS E VELOCIDADES DA AGUA}

G_aq_canal = r_m_aq/(rNc_ev_eq*A_canal_ag)
G_af_canal = r_m_af/(rNc_cond_eq*A_canal_ag)

v_aq_canal_max = G_aq_canal/rho_aq_min
v_af_canal_max = G_af_canal/rho_af_min

Margem_v_aq = v_canal_ref-v_aq_canal_max
Margem_v_af = v_canal_ref-v_af_canal_max

{38. DIAGNOSTICO DO LADO DA AMONIA}

G_am_ev = 1/(rNc_ev_eq*A_canal_am)
G_am_cond = 1/(rNc_cond_eq*A_canal_am)

v_esp_am_ev_out = Volume(Ammonia,P=P_ev,x=1)
v_esp_am_cond_in = Volume(Ammonia,P=P_cond,h=h[4])

v_am_ev_out = G_am_ev*v_esp_am_ev_out
v_am_cond_in = G_am_cond*v_esp_am_cond_in

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 6 - PERDAS NOS CANAIS DE AGUA}

{39. UNIDADES DAS VARIAVEIS PRINCIPAIS}
$VarInfo mu_aq Units='Pa-s'
$VarInfo mu_af Units='Pa-s'
$VarInfo Re_aq_PHE Units='-'
$VarInfo Re_af_PHE Units='-'
$VarInfo fF_aq_PHE Units='-'
$VarInfo fF_af_PHE Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_aq_PHE Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_af_PHE Units='-'
$VarInfo DP_ev_canal Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_cond_canal Units='kPa'
$VarInfo w_ev_canal_el Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo w_cond_canal_el Units='kJ/kg'

{40. PREPARACAO DOS DOIS CIRCUITOS}
T_sw_hyd[1] = (T_aq_in+T_aq_out)/2
T_sw_hyd[2] = (T_af_in+T_af_out)/2
SP_mu[1] = SP_aq
SP_mu[2] = SP_af
G_sw_hyd[1] = G_aq_canal
G_sw_hyd[2] = G_af_canal
rho_sw_hyd[1] = rho_aq_med
rho_sw_hyd[2] = rho_af_med
r_m_sw[1] = r_m_aq
r_m_sw[2] = r_m_af
eta_sw_hyd[1] = eta_b_aq*eta_m_aq
eta_sw_hyd[2] = eta_b_af*eta_m_af
mu_ref = 1 [Pa-s]
beta_Martin = beta_PHE
beta_M_num = beta_Martin/(1 [deg])

{41. VISCOSIDADE E REYNOLDS}

Duplicate k=1,2
  tau_mu[k] = (T_sw_hyd[k]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  SR_mu[k] = 35.16504 [g/kg]*SP_mu[k]/35
  wS_mu[k] = SR_mu[k]/(1000 [g/kg])

  A_mu[k] = 1.540913604+0.019981117208*tau_mu[k]-0.000095203865864*tau_mu[k]^2
  B_mu[k] = 7.9739318223-0.075614568881*tau_mu[k]+0.00047237011074*tau_mu[k]^2

  mu_water[k] = mu_ref*(0.000042844324477+1/(0.15700386464*(tau_mu[k]+64.99262005)^2-91.296496657))
  mu_sw[k] = mu_water[k]*(1+A_mu[k]*wS_mu[k]+B_mu[k]*wS_mu[k]^2)
  Re_sw[k] = G_sw_hyd[k]*Dh_ag/mu_sw[k]

  {42. MARTIN: RAMOS DA CORRELACAO, NAO MODELO DE TUBO LISO}

  fF_sw[k] = OTEC_fF(Re_sw[k],beta_M_num)
  fD_sw[k] = 4*fF_sw[k]

  {43. PERDA DISTRIBUIDA NOS CANAIS, EM kPa}

  DP_sw_canal[k] = fD_sw[k]*(L_placa/Dh_ag)*G_sw_hyd[k]^2/(2*rho_sw_hyd[k])*Convert(Pa,kPa)

  w_sw_canal_el[k] = DP_sw_canal[k]*(r_m_sw[k]/rho_sw_hyd[k])/eta_sw_hyd[k]
End

{44. INDICADORES HIDRAULICOS}
mu_aq = mu_sw[1]
mu_af = mu_sw[2]
Re_aq_PHE = Re_sw[1]
Re_af_PHE = Re_sw[2]
fF_aq_PHE = fF_sw[1]
fF_af_PHE = fF_sw[2]
fD_aq_PHE = fD_sw[1]
fD_af_PHE = fD_sw[2]
DP_ev_canal = DP_sw_canal[1]
DP_cond_canal = DP_sw_canal[2]
w_ev_canal_el = w_sw_canal_el[1]
w_cond_canal_el = w_sw_canal_el[2]

{45. CENARIOS DE SENSIBILIDADE, NAO INTERVALOS DE CONFIANCA}
DP_ev_canal_inf = 0.5*DP_ev_canal
DP_ev_canal_sup = 2*DP_ev_canal
DP_cond_canal_inf = 0.5*DP_cond_canal
DP_cond_canal_sup = 2*DP_cond_canal

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 7 - COEFICIENTES DE TRANSFERENCIA DE CALOR}

{46. UNIDADES E ESTIMATIVA INICIAL DO NOVO COEFICIENTE}
$VarInfo U_pre_ev Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo R_U_pre Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo R_U_pre_rel Units='-'
$VarInfo h_aq_M Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo h_af_M Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo h_am_pre_M Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo U_lim_ev_limpo Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo U_lim_cond_limpo Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo h_am_boil_req Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo h_am_cond_req Units='kW/m^2-K'
$VarInfo R_placa Units='m^2-K/kW'
$VarInfo R_disp_boil Units='m^2-K/kW'
$VarInfo R_disp_cond Units='m^2-K/kW'
$VarInfo DT_sub_am_pre Units='DELTAC'

{47. CENARIO DE MATERIAL E INCRUSTACAO}

k_placa_Ti = 16.4 [W/m-K]
R_placa = t_placa/(k_placa_Ti*Convert(W,kW))
Rf_aq_pre = 0 [m^2-K/kW]
Rf_am_pre = 0 [m^2-K/kW]

{48. CONDUTIVIDADE DA AGUA DO MAR - IAPWS G10-15 (2015)}

p_U = P_amb/(1000 [kPa])
a1_U = -0.00007180891+0.0000001831971*p_U
a2_U = 0.001048077-0.000004494722*p_U
b1_U = 0.1463375+0.0009208586*p_U
b2_U = -0.003086908+0.00001798489*p_U
cp_sw_U[1] = cp_aq_ef
cp_sw_U[2] = cp_af_ef

Duplicate n=1,2
  a_U[n] = a1_U*EXP(a2_U*tau_mu[n])
  b_U[n] = b1_U*EXP(b2_U*tau_mu[n])
  k_water_U[n] = Conductivity(Water,T=T_sw_hyd[n],P=P_amb)
  k_sw_U[n] = k_water_U[n]+1 [W/m-K]*a_U[n]*(1000*wS_mu[n])^(1+b_U[n])

  Pr_sw_U[n] = cp_sw_U[n]*Convert(kJ,J)*mu_sw[n]/k_sw_U[n]
  Nu_sw_U[n] = OTEC_Nu(Re_sw[n],Pr_sw_U[n],beta_M_num)
  h_sw_U[n] = Nu_sw_U[n]*k_sw_U[n]/Dh_ag*Convert(W,kW)
End

h_aq_M = h_sw_U[1]
h_af_M = h_sw_U[2]

{49. AMONIA LIQUIDA NO PRE-AQUECIMENTO}

T_am_pre_med = (T[2]+T_ev)/2
DT_sub_am_pre = T_ev-T_am_pre_med
mu_am_pre = Viscosity(Ammonia,T=T_am_pre_med,P=P_ev)
k_am_pre = Conductivity(Ammonia,T=T_am_pre_med,P=P_ev)
cp_am_pre = Cp(Ammonia,T=T_am_pre_med,P=P_ev)

Re_am_pre = G_am_ev*Dh_am/mu_am_pre
Pr_am_pre = cp_am_pre*Convert(kJ,J)*mu_am_pre/k_am_pre

fF_am_pre = OTEC_fF(Re_am_pre,beta_M_num)
Nu_am_pre = OTEC_Nu(Re_am_pre,Pr_am_pre,beta_M_num)
h_am_pre_M = Nu_am_pre*k_am_pre/Dh_am*Convert(W,kW)

{50. FECHO DO U DE PRE-AQUECIMENTO}

Duplicate mU=1,N_pre
  C_pre_U_seg[mU] = (q_pre_ev/N_pre)/DTlm_pre[mU]*Convert(kJ,J)
End
C_pre_U = SUM(C_pre_U_seg[1..N_pre])
cp_aq_U_SI = cp_sw_U[1]*Convert(kJ,J)
cp_am_U_SI = cp_am_pre*Convert(kJ,J)
Rt_U_SI = t_placa/k_placa_Ti+(Rf_aq_pre+Rf_am_pre)*Convert(W,kW)

Cpre_num = C_pre_U/(1 [J/kg-K])
Rb_num = rA_boil_ev/(1 [m^2-s/kg])
Nv_num = rNc_ev_vel/(1 [s/kg])
Ap_num = A_placa_ef/(1 [m^2])
Aw_num = A_canal_ag/(1 [m^2])
Aa_num = A_canal_am/(1 [m^2])
Dw_num = Dh_ag/(1 [m])
Da_num = Dh_am/(1 [m])
rm_num = r_m_aq
muw_num = mu_aq/(1 [Pa-s])
kw_num = k_sw_U[1]/(1 [W/m-K])
cpw_num = cp_aq_U_SI/(1 [J/kg-K])
mua_num = mu_am_pre/(1 [Pa-s])
ka_num = k_am_pre/(1 [W/m-K])
cpa_num = cp_am_U_SI/(1 [J/kg-K])
Rt_num = Rt_U_SI/(1 [m^2-K/W])
U_pre_num = OTEC_Usolve(Cpre_num,Rb_num,Nv_num,Ap_num,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,rm_num,muw_num,kw_num,cpw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,beta_M_num)
U_pre_ev = U_pre_num*1 [W/m^2-K]*Convert(W,kW)
R_U_pre = U_pre_ev-1/(1/h_aq_M+R_placa+1/h_am_pre_M+Rf_aq_pre+Rf_am_pre)
R_U_pre_rel = ABS(R_U_pre)/U_pre_ev
F_U_pre_antigo = U_pre_ref_antigo/U_pre_ev

{51. CONDICOES NECESSARIAS PARA AS ZONAS BIFASICAS}

U_lim_ev_limpo = 1/(1/h_aq_M+R_placa)
U_lim_cond_limpo = 1/(1/h_af_M+R_placa)
F_lim_boil = U_boil_ev/U_lim_ev_limpo
F_lim_cond = U_phase_cond/U_lim_cond_limpo

R_disp_boil = 1/U_boil_ev-1/h_aq_M-R_placa
R_disp_cond = 1/U_phase_cond-1/h_af_M-R_placa

h_am_boil_req = 1/R_disp_boil
h_am_cond_req = 1/R_disp_cond

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 8 - CONTROLO DO MODELO BIFASICO}

{52. UNIDADES EXPLICITAS}
$VarInfo hfg_ev Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo hfg_cond Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo q_flux_boil Units='kW/m^2'
$VarInfo q_flux_cond Units='kW/m^2'
$VarInfo Bo_ev Units='-'
$VarInfo x_ev_in Units='-'
$VarInfo x_ev_out Units='-'
$VarInfo x_cond_in Units='-'
$VarInfo x_cond_out Units='-'
$VarInfo x_cond_med Units='-'
$VarInfo R_lat_ev Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo R_lat_cond Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo R_lat_cond_rel Units='-'
$VarInfo Faixa_G_Koyama Units='-'
$VarInfo Faixa_P_Koyama Units='-'
$VarInfo Faixa_q_Koyama Units='-'
$VarInfo Faixa_b_Koyama Units='-'
$VarInfo Geometria_Koyama_conferida Units='-'
$VarInfo Cobertura_Koyama Units='-'
$VarInfo Excesso_P_Koyama Units='-'
$VarInfo Faixa_G_Tao Units='-'
$VarInfo Faixa_P_Tao Units='-'
$VarInfo Faixa_xmed_Tao Units='-'
$VarInfo Geometria_Tao_conferida Units='-'
$VarInfo Cobertura_Tao Units='-'
$VarInfo rNc_cond_G21 Units='s/kg'
$VarInfo rA_cond_G21 Units='m^2-s/kg'
$VarInfo F_area_cond_G21 Units='-'

{53. CARGAS LATENTES E FLUXOS MEDIOS DE CALOR}

hfg_ev = h[3]-h_ev_liq
hfg_cond = h_cond_vap-h[1]
q_flux_boil = q_boil_ev/rA_boil_ev
q_flux_cond = q_cond/rA_cond
Bo_ev = q_flux_boil/(G_am_ev*hfg_ev)

{54. TITULOS E BALANCOS}

x_ev_in = 0
x_ev_out = 1
x_cond_in = x_4
x_cond_out = 0
x_cond_med = (x_cond_in+x_cond_out)/2

R_lat_ev = q_boil_ev-(x_ev_out-x_ev_in)*hfg_ev
R_lat_cond = q_cond-(x_cond_in-x_cond_out)*hfg_cond
R_lat_cond_rel = ABS(R_lat_cond)/ABS(q_cond)

{55. FONTE DE EBULICAO: KOYAMA ET AL. (2014)}

G_Koyama_min = 5 [kg/m^2-s]
G_Koyama_max = 7.5 [kg/m^2-s]
P_Koyama_min = 700 [kPa]
P_Koyama_max = 900 [kPa]
q_Koyama_min = 10 [kW/m^2]
q_Koyama_max = 20 [kW/m^2]
b_Koyama_min = 0.001 [m]
b_Koyama_max = 0.005 [m]

Faixa_G_Koyama = IF(G_am_ev,G_Koyama_min,0,1,1)*IF(G_am_ev,G_Koyama_max,1,1,0)
Faixa_P_Koyama = IF(P_ev,P_Koyama_min,0,1,1)*IF(P_ev,P_Koyama_max,1,1,0)
Faixa_q_Koyama = IF(q_flux_boil,q_Koyama_min,0,1,1)*IF(q_flux_boil,q_Koyama_max,1,1,0)
Faixa_b_Koyama = IF(b_am,b_Koyama_min,0,1,1)*IF(b_am,b_Koyama_max,1,1,0)
Excesso_P_Koyama = MAX(0,P_ev/P_Koyama_max-1)

Geometria_Koyama_conferida = 0
Cobertura_Koyama = Faixa_G_Koyama*Faixa_P_Koyama*Faixa_q_Koyama*Faixa_b_Koyama*Geometria_Koyama_conferida

{56. FONTE DE CONDENSACAO: TAO ET AL. (2020)}

G_Tao_min = 21 [kg/m^2-s]
G_Tao_max = 78 [kg/m^2-s]
P_Tao_min = 630 [kPa]
P_Tao_max = 930 [kPa]
x_Tao_min = 0.05
x_Tao_max = 0.65

Faixa_G_Tao = IF(G_am_cond,G_Tao_min,0,1,1)*IF(G_am_cond,G_Tao_max,1,1,0)
Faixa_P_Tao = IF(P_cond,P_Tao_min,0,1,1)*IF(P_cond,P_Tao_max,1,1,0)
Faixa_xmed_Tao = IF(x_cond_med,x_Tao_min,0,1,1)*IF(x_cond_med,x_Tao_max,1,1,0)
Geometria_Tao_conferida = 0
Cobertura_Tao = Faixa_G_Tao*Faixa_P_Tao*Faixa_xmed_Tao*Geometria_Tao_conferida

{57. DIAGNOSTICO DE AREA PARA G=21 NO CONDENSADOR}

rNc_cond_G21 = 1/(G_Tao_min*A_canal_am)
rA_cond_G21 = 2*A_placa_ef*rNc_cond_G21
F_area_cond_G21 = rA_cond/rA_cond_G21

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 9 - CAPACIDADE E ATRITO DAS TUBAGENS}

{58. UNIDADES EXPLICITAS}
$VarInfo Dext_CWP Units='m'
$VarInfo e_CWP Units='m'
$VarInfo Dint_CWP Units='m'
$VarInfo Dint_aq_tubo Units='m'
$VarInfo A_CWP Units='m^2'
$VarInfo A_aq_tubo Units='m^2'
$VarInfo mu_af_tubo Units='Pa-s'
$VarInfo mu_aq_tubo Units='Pa-s'
$VarInfo Re_CWP_ref Units='-'
$VarInfo Re_aq_tubo_ref Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_CWP_ref Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_aq_tubo_ref Units='-'
$VarInfo DP_din_CWP_ref Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_din_aq_ref Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_dist_CWP_ref Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_dist_aq_ref Units='kPa'
$VarInfo Vdot_CWP_ref Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_aq_tubo_ref Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo m_am_cap_CWP Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo m_am_cap_aq Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo w_sem_rede_adicional Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo m_am_lim_inf Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_af_lim_inf Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_aq_lim_inf Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo N_CWP_lim_inf Units='-'
$VarInfo N_aq_lim_inf Units='-'
$VarInfo R_Colebrook_CWP Units='-'
$VarInfo R_Colebrook_aq Units='-'

{59. GEOMETRIA HERDADA DO MODELO ORIGINAL}

Dext_CWP = 3.26 [m]
DR_CWP = 26
e_CWP = Dext_CWP/DR_CWP
Dint_CWP = Dext_CWP-2*e_CWP
A_CWP = PI*Dint_CWP^2/4

Dint_aq_tubo = 3.26 [m]
A_aq_tubo = PI*Dint_aq_tubo^2/4
epsilon_HDPE = 0.0000015 [m]
epsrel_CWP = epsilon_HDPE/Dint_CWP
epsrel_aq_tubo = epsilon_HDPE/Dint_aq_tubo

{60. COMPRIMENTOS DE CENARIO}

z_topo_CWP = 0 [m]
H_vertical_CWP = z_af_dados-z_topo_CWP
L_extra_CWP = 0 [m]
L_CWP_cenario = H_vertical_CWP+L_extra_CWP
L_aq_cenario = 100 [m]
Tracado_confirmado = 0

{61. PROPRIEDADES ANTES DOS PERMUTADORES}

T_af_tubo_num = (T_af_in-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
T_aq_tubo_num = (T_aq_in-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
mu_af_tubo = OTEC_muSW_tubo(T_af_tubo_num,SP_af)*1 [Pa-s]
mu_aq_tubo = OTEC_muSW_tubo(T_aq_tubo_num,SP_aq)*1 [Pa-s]
rho_af_tubo_ref = rho_af_in
rho_aq_tubo_ref = rho_aq_in

{62. CAPACIDADE POR TUBAGEM A VELOCIDADE DE REFERENCIA}

v_CWP_ref = v_tubo_max
v_aq_tubo_ref = v_tubo_max
Vdot_CWP_ref = v_CWP_ref*A_CWP
Vdot_aq_tubo_ref = v_aq_tubo_ref*A_aq_tubo
m_am_cap_CWP = Vdot_CWP_ref/r_V_af
m_am_cap_aq = Vdot_aq_tubo_ref/r_V_aq

{63. ATRITO DISTRIBUIDO POR TUBAGEM}

Re_CWP_ref = rho_af_tubo_ref*v_CWP_ref*Dint_CWP/mu_af_tubo
Re_aq_tubo_ref = rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq_tubo_ref*Dint_aq_tubo/mu_aq_tubo
fD_CWP_ref = OTEC_fD_tubo(Re_CWP_ref,epsrel_CWP)
fD_aq_tubo_ref = OTEC_fD_tubo(Re_aq_tubo_ref,epsrel_aq_tubo)

DP_din_CWP_ref = rho_af_tubo_ref*v_CWP_ref^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_din_aq_ref = rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq_tubo_ref^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_dist_CWP_ref = fD_CWP_ref*(L_CWP_cenario/Dint_CWP)*DP_din_CWP_ref
DP_dist_aq_ref = fD_aq_tubo_ref*(L_aq_cenario/Dint_aq_tubo)*DP_din_aq_ref

R_Colebrook_CWP = 1/SQRT(fD_CWP_ref)+2*LOG10(epsrel_CWP/3.7+2.51/(Re_CWP_ref*SQRT(fD_CWP_ref)))
R_Colebrook_aq = 1/SQRT(fD_aq_tubo_ref)+2*LOG10(epsrel_aq_tubo/3.7+2.51/(Re_aq_tubo_ref*SQRT(fD_aq_tubo_ref)))

{64. LIMITE INFERIOR DO CAUDAL DE AMONIA}

w_sem_rede_adicional = w_ger-w_b_am_el-w_ev_canal_el-w_cond_canal_el
m_am_lim_inf = P_liq_meta/w_sem_rede_adicional
Vdot_af_lim_inf = r_V_af*m_am_lim_inf
Vdot_aq_lim_inf = r_V_aq*m_am_lim_inf
N_CWP_lim_inf = CEIL(Vdot_af_lim_inf/Vdot_CWP_ref)
N_aq_lim_inf = CEIL(Vdot_aq_lim_inf/Vdot_aq_tubo_ref)

$VarInfo m10_n Units='-'
$VarInfo N_CWP_cenario Units='-'
$VarInfo N_aq_cenario Units='-'
$VarInfo m_am_cap_cenario Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo m_am_parcial Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_af_parcial Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_aq_parcial Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo v_CWP_parcial Units='m/s'
$VarInfo v_aq_parcial Units='m/s'
$VarInfo Margem_v_CWP_parcial Units='m/s'
$VarInfo Margem_v_aq_parcial Units='m/s'
$VarInfo Re_CWP_parcial Units='-'
$VarInfo Re_aq_parcial Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_CWP_parcial Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_aq_parcial Units='-'
$VarInfo DP_CWP_parcial Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_aq_parcial Units='kPa'
$VarInfo Fraccao_bombas_parcial Units='-'
$VarInfo Fecho_total_concluido Units='-'
$VarInfo P_af_tubo_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_aq_tubo_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_ger_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_b_am_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_ev_canais_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_cond_canais_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_bombas_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_liq_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo R_meta_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_af_func_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_aq_func_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo R_Paf_parcial Units='kW'
$VarInfo R_Paq_parcial Units='kW'

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 10 - BALANCO PARCIAL COM ATRITO TUBULAR}

{65. CENARIO DE RAMOS PARALELOS}
N_CWP_cenario = 2
N_aq_cenario = 2
eta_af_tubo = eta_b_af*eta_m_af
eta_aq_tubo = eta_b_aq*eta_m_aq
m_am_cap_cenario = MIN(N_CWP_cenario*A_CWP*v_tubo_max/r_V_af,N_aq_cenario*A_aq_tubo*v_tubo_max/r_V_aq)

{66. CONTRATO NUMERICO DAS FUNCOES}
$VarInfo P10_n Units='-'
P10_n = P_liq_meta/(1 [kW])
$VarInfo w10_n Units='-'
w10_n = w_sem_rede_adicional/(1 [kJ/kg])
$VarInfo mmax10_n Units='-'
mmax10_n = m_am_cap_cenario/(1 [kg/s])
$VarInfo rvC10_n Units='-'
rvC10_n = r_V_af/(1 [m^3/kg])
$VarInfo rvH10_n Units='-'
rvH10_n = r_V_aq/(1 [m^3/kg])
$VarInfo aC10_n Units='-'
aC10_n = A_CWP/(1 [m^2])
$VarInfo aH10_n Units='-'
aH10_n = A_aq_tubo/(1 [m^2])
$VarInfo dC10_n Units='-'
dC10_n = Dint_CWP/(1 [m])
$VarInfo dH10_n Units='-'
dH10_n = Dint_aq_tubo/(1 [m])
$VarInfo lC10_n Units='-'
lC10_n = L_CWP_cenario/(1 [m])
$VarInfo lH10_n Units='-'
lH10_n = L_aq_cenario/(1 [m])
$VarInfo rhC10_n Units='-'
rhC10_n = rho_af_tubo_ref/(1 [kg/m^3])
$VarInfo rhH10_n Units='-'
rhH10_n = rho_aq_tubo_ref/(1 [kg/m^3])
$VarInfo muC10_n Units='-'
muC10_n = mu_af_tubo/(1 [Pa-s])
$VarInfo muH10_n Units='-'
muH10_n = mu_aq_tubo/(1 [Pa-s])

{67. CAUDAL PARA ATINGIR A META NO BALANCO PARCIAL}
m10_n = OTEC_mparcial(P10_n,w10_n,mmax10_n,rvC10_n,rvH10_n,N_CWP_cenario,N_aq_cenario,aC10_n,aH10_n,dC10_n,dH10_n,lC10_n,lH10_n,rhC10_n,rhH10_n,muC10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_CWP,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_af_tubo,eta_aq_tubo)
m_am_parcial = m10_n*1 [kg/s]
Vdot_af_parcial = m_am_parcial*r_V_af
Vdot_aq_parcial = m_am_parcial*r_V_aq
v_CWP_parcial = Vdot_af_parcial/(N_CWP_cenario*A_CWP)
v_aq_parcial = Vdot_aq_parcial/(N_aq_cenario*A_aq_tubo)
Margem_v_CWP_parcial = v_tubo_max-v_CWP_parcial
Margem_v_aq_parcial = v_tubo_max-v_aq_parcial

{68. PERDAS EM UM RAMO; POTENCIA PARA O CONJUNTO DOS RAMOS}
Re_CWP_parcial = rho_af_tubo_ref*v_CWP_parcial*Dint_CWP/mu_af_tubo
Re_aq_parcial = rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq_parcial*Dint_aq_tubo/mu_aq_tubo
fD_CWP_parcial = OTEC_fD_tubo(Re_CWP_parcial,epsrel_CWP)
fD_aq_parcial = OTEC_fD_tubo(Re_aq_parcial,epsrel_aq_tubo)
DP_CWP_parcial = fD_CWP_parcial*(L_CWP_cenario/Dint_CWP)*rho_af_tubo_ref*v_CWP_parcial^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_aq_parcial = fD_aq_parcial*(L_aq_cenario/Dint_aq_tubo)*rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq_parcial^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)
P_af_tubo_parcial = DP_CWP_parcial*Vdot_af_parcial/eta_af_tubo
P_aq_tubo_parcial = DP_aq_parcial*Vdot_aq_parcial/eta_aq_tubo

{69. BALANCO ELECTRICO - SEM DUPLICAR AS PARCELAS DOS CANAIS}
P_ger_parcial = m_am_parcial*w_ger
P_b_am_parcial = m_am_parcial*w_b_am_el
P_ev_canais_parcial = m_am_parcial*w_ev_canal_el
P_cond_canais_parcial = m_am_parcial*w_cond_canal_el
P_bombas_parcial = P_b_am_parcial+P_ev_canais_parcial+P_cond_canais_parcial+P_af_tubo_parcial+P_aq_tubo_parcial
P_liq_parcial = P_ger_parcial-P_bombas_parcial
R_meta_parcial = P_liq_parcial-P_liq_meta
Fraccao_bombas_parcial = P_bombas_parcial/P_ger_parcial

{70. CONFERENCIA CRUZADA DAS FUNCOES E DO PROGRAMA PRINCIPAL}
P_af_func_parcial = OTEC_Ptubo_num(m10_n,rvC10_n,N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,rhC10_n,muC10_n,epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo)*1 [kW]
P_aq_func_parcial = OTEC_Ptubo_num(m10_n,rvH10_n,N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,rhH10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo)*1 [kW]
R_Paf_parcial = P_af_func_parcial-P_af_tubo_parcial
R_Paq_parcial = P_aq_func_parcial-P_aq_tubo_parcial
Fecho_total_concluido = 0

$VarInfo g11 Units='m/s^2'
$VarInfo Delta_rho11 Units='kg/m^3'
$VarInfo w_col_af11 Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo w_col_aq11 Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo w_disp11 Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo m_am_cenario11 Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo Acrescimo_m11 Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_af11 Units='m^3/s'

$VarInfo v_af11 Units='m/s'
$VarInfo v_aq11 Units='m/s'
$VarInfo Margem_v_af11 Units='m/s'
$VarInfo Margem_v_aq11 Units='m/s'
$VarInfo DP_col_envelope11 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_col_af11 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_col_aq11 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_atrito_af11 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_atrito_aq11 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_local_af11 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_local_aq11 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo P_atrito_af11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_atrito_aq11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_local_af11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_local_aq11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_col_af11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_col_aq11 Units='kW'

$VarInfo P_canais_ev11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_canais_cond11 Units='kW'

$VarInfo P_liq_cenario11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo R_meta11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_omitida_no_ponto10_11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_liq_mantendo_m10_11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_rede_func_af11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_rede_func_aq11 Units='kW'
$VarInfo R_Paf11 Units='kW'

$VarInfo K_ent_af11 Units='-'
$VarInfo K_plenum_af11 Units='-'
$VarInfo K_ent_aq11 Units='-'
$VarInfo K_plenum_aq11 Units='-'
$VarInfo K_af11 Units='-'
$VarInfo K_aq11 Units='-'
$VarInfo expo_perfil11 Units='-'
$VarInfo fraccao_col11 Units='-'
$VarInfo w11_n Units='-'
$VarInfo m11_n Units='-'
$VarInfo Re_af11 Units='-'
$VarInfo Re_aq11 Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_af11 Units='-'
$VarInfo fD_aq11 Units='-'

$VarInfo Fecho_definitivo11 Units='-'
$VarInfo Geometria_local_confirmada11 Units='-'
$VarInfo Perfil_medido_disponivel11 Units='-'

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 11 - PERDAS LOCAIS E COLUNA POR CENARIO}

{71. PERDAS LOCAIS DE CENARIO - VELOCIDADE NA MESMA CONDUTA}
K_ent_af11 = 0.5
K_plenum_af11 = 1
K_ent_aq11 = 0.5
K_plenum_aq11 = 1
K_af11 = K_ent_af11+K_plenum_af11
K_aq11 = K_ent_aq11+K_plenum_aq11
Geometria_local_confirmada11 = 0

{72. COLUNA FRIA - APROXIMACAO DE DENSIDADES REFERIDAS A PRESSAO COMUM}
g11 = 9.80665 [m/s^2]
expo_perfil11 = 1
fraccao_col11 = OTEC_colfrac11(expo_perfil11)
Delta_rho11 = rho_af_in-rho_aq_in
DP_col_envelope11 = g11*Delta_rho11*H_vertical_CWP*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_col_af11 = DP_col_envelope11*fraccao_col11
DP_col_aq11 = 0 [kPa]
Perfil_medido_disponivel11 = 0
w_col_af11 = DP_col_af11*r_V_af/eta_af_tubo
w_col_aq11 = DP_col_aq11*r_V_aq/eta_aq_tubo
w_disp11 = w_sem_rede_adicional-w_col_af11-w_col_aq11
w11_n = w_disp11/(1 [kJ/kg])

{73. FECHO DO CAUDAL - DUAS CONDUTAS POR CIRCUITO, CENARIO DA PARTE 10}
m11_n = OTEC_mrede11(P10_n,w11_n,mmax10_n,rvC10_n,rvH10_n,N_CWP_cenario,N_aq_cenario,aC10_n,aH10_n,dC10_n,dH10_n,lC10_n,lH10_n,rhC10_n,rhH10_n,muC10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_CWP,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_af_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_af11,K_aq11)
m_am_cenario11 = m11_n*1 [kg/s]
Vdot_af11 = m_am_cenario11*r_V_af
Vdot_aq11 = m_am_cenario11*r_V_aq
v_af11 = Vdot_af11/(N_CWP_cenario*A_CWP)
v_aq11 = Vdot_aq11/(N_aq_cenario*A_aq_tubo)
Margem_v_af11 = v_tubo_max-v_af11
Margem_v_aq11 = v_tubo_max-v_aq11
Re_af11 = rho_af_tubo_ref*v_af11*Dint_CWP/mu_af_tubo
Re_aq11 = rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq11*Dint_aq_tubo/mu_aq_tubo
fD_af11 = OTEC_fD_tubo(Re_af11,epsrel_CWP)
fD_aq11 = OTEC_fD_tubo(Re_aq11,epsrel_aq_tubo)
DP_atrito_af11 = fD_af11*(L_CWP_cenario/Dint_CWP)*rho_af_tubo_ref*v_af11^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_atrito_aq11 = fD_aq11*(L_aq_cenario/Dint_aq_tubo)*rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq11^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_local_af11 = K_af11*rho_af_tubo_ref*v_af11^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_local_aq11 = K_aq11*rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq11^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa)

{74. POTENCIAS ELECTRICAS POR PARCELA E PARA TODOS OS RAMOS}
P_atrito_af11 = DP_atrito_af11*Vdot_af11/eta_af_tubo
P_atrito_aq11 = DP_atrito_aq11*Vdot_aq11/eta_aq_tubo
P_local_af11 = DP_local_af11*Vdot_af11/eta_af_tubo
P_local_aq11 = DP_local_aq11*Vdot_aq11/eta_aq_tubo
P_col_af11 = DP_col_af11*Vdot_af11/eta_af_tubo
P_col_aq11 = DP_col_aq11*Vdot_aq11/eta_aq_tubo
P_ger11 = m_am_cenario11*w_ger
P_b_am11 = m_am_cenario11*w_b_am_el
P_canais_ev11 = m_am_cenario11*w_ev_canal_el
P_canais_cond11 = m_am_cenario11*w_cond_canal_el
P_consumos11 = P_b_am11+P_canais_ev11+P_canais_cond11+P_atrito_af11+P_atrito_aq11+P_local_af11+P_local_aq11+P_col_af11+P_col_aq11
P_liq_cenario11 = P_ger11-P_consumos11
R_meta11 = P_liq_cenario11-P_liq_meta
Fraccao_consumos11 = P_consumos11/P_ger11
Acrescimo_m11 = m_am_cenario11-m_am_parcial
P_omitida_no_ponto10_11 = (DP_col_af11+K_af11*rho_af_tubo_ref*v_CWP_parcial^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa))*Vdot_af_parcial/eta_af_tubo+(DP_col_aq11+K_aq11*rho_aq_tubo_ref*v_aq_parcial^2/2*Convert(Pa,kPa))*Vdot_aq_parcial/eta_aq_tubo
P_liq_mantendo_m10_11 = P_liq_parcial-P_omitida_no_ponto10_11
Fecho_definitivo11 = 0

{75. CONFERENCIA CRUZADA DAS POTENCIAS}
P_rede_func_af11 = OTEC_Prede11(m11_n,rvC10_n,N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,rhC10_n,muC10_n,epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo,K_af11)*1 [kW]
P_rede_func_aq11 = OTEC_Prede11(m11_n,rvH10_n,N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,rhH10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_aq11)*1 [kW]
R_Paf11 = P_rede_func_af11-P_atrito_af11-P_local_af11
R_Paq11 = P_rede_func_aq11-P_atrito_aq11-P_local_aq11

$VarInfo Nc_agua_mod12 Units='-'
$VarInfo Nc_am_mod12 Units='-'
$VarInfo Np_pacote_mod12 Units='-'
$VarInfo Np_term_mod12 Units='-'

$VarInfo G_af12 Units='kg/m^2-s'

$VarInfo h_af12 Units='kW/m^2-K'

$VarInfo U_pre12 Units='kW/m^2-K'

$VarInfo P_canais_cond12 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_rede_af12 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_rede_aq12 Units='kW'

$VarInfo P_liq12 Units='kW'
