$VarInfo R_Pev12 Units='kW'
$VarInfo R_Pcond12 Units='kW'
$VarInfo DP_canais_ev12 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo DP_canais_cond12 Units='kPa'
$VarInfo w_sem_canais12 Units='kJ/kg'
$VarInfo m_am12 Units='kg/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_af12 Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo Vdot_aq12 Units='m^3/s'
$VarInfo rA_pre12 Units='m^2-s/kg'

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 12 - MODULOS INTEIROS E META DE 10000 kW}

{76. MODULO DE CENARIO E CONTAGEM DAS EXTREMIDADES}
Nc_agua_mod12 = 200
Nc_am_mod12 = Nc_agua_mod12
Np_pacote_mod12 = Nc_agua_mod12+Nc_am_mod12+1
Np_term_mod12 = 2
Np_act_mod12 = Np_pacote_mod12-Np_term_mod12
A_mod12 = Np_act_mod12*A_placa_ef

{77. CONTAGENS INTEIRAS, FIXADAS ANTES DO REAJUSTE}
Nmod_ev12 = CEIL(MAX(m_am_cenario11*rA_ev/A_mod12,m_am_cenario11*r_m_aq/(rho_aq_min*v_canal_ref*Nc_agua_mod12*A_canal_ag)))
Nmod_cond12 = CEIL(MAX(m_am_cenario11*rA_cond/A_mod12,m_am_cenario11*r_m_af/(rho_af_min*v_canal_ref*Nc_agua_mod12*A_canal_ag)))
Nc_ev12 = Nmod_ev12*Nc_agua_mod12
Nc_cond12 = Nmod_cond12*Nc_agua_mod12
Nc_am_ev12 = Nmod_ev12*Nc_am_mod12
Nc_am_cond12 = Nmod_cond12*Nc_am_mod12
Np_ev12 = Nmod_ev12*Np_pacote_mod12
Np_cond12 = Nmod_cond12*Np_pacote_mod12
Np_total12 = Np_ev12+Np_cond12
Np_act_total12 = (Nmod_ev12+Nmod_cond12)*Np_act_mod12
Np_term_total12 = (Nmod_ev12+Nmod_cond12)*Np_term_mod12
A_ev_inst12 = Nmod_ev12*A_mod12
A_cond_inst12 = Nmod_cond12*A_mod12

{78. NOVO BALANCO - CANAIS DA PARTE 11 SUBSTITUIDOS, NAO SOMADOS}
w_sem_canais12 = w_ger-w_b_am_el-w_col_af11-w_col_aq11
w12_n = w_sem_canais12/(1 [kJ/kg])
lp12_n = L_placa/(1 [m])
rmE12_n = r_m_aq
rmC12_n = r_m_af
rhE12_n = rho_aq_med/(1 [kg/m^3])
rhCo12_n = rho_af_med/(1 [kg/m^3])
muE12_n = mu_aq/(1 [Pa-s])
muCo12_n = mu_af/(1 [Pa-s])
m12_n = OTEC_mdisc12(P10_n,w12_n,mmax10_n,rvC10_n,rvH10_n,N_CWP_cenario,N_aq_cenario,aC10_n,aH10_n,dC10_n,dH10_n,lC10_n,lH10_n,rhC10_n,rhH10_n,muC10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_CWP,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_af_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_af11,K_aq11,Nc_ev12,Nc_cond12,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rmE12_n,rmC12_n,rhE12_n,rhCo12_n,muE12_n,muCo12_n,beta_M_num)
m_am12 = m12_n*1 [kg/s]
Vdot_af12 = m_am12*r_V_af
Vdot_aq12 = m_am12*r_V_aq
v_af_tubo12 = Vdot_af12/(N_CWP_cenario*A_CWP)
v_aq_tubo12 = Vdot_aq12/(N_aq_cenario*A_aq_tubo)
G_aq12 = m_am12*r_m_aq/(Nc_ev12*A_canal_ag)
G_af12 = m_am12*r_m_af/(Nc_cond12*A_canal_ag)
G_am_ev12 = m_am12/(Nc_am_ev12*A_canal_am)
G_am_cond12 = m_am12/(Nc_am_cond12*A_canal_am)
v_aq_canal12 = G_aq12/rho_aq_min
v_af_canal12 = G_af12/rho_af_min
Margem_v_af_tubo12 = v_tubo_max-v_af_tubo12
Margem_v_aq_tubo12 = v_tubo_max-v_aq_tubo12
Margem_v_ev12 = v_canal_ref-v_aq_canal12
Margem_v_cond12 = v_canal_ref-v_af_canal12

{79. PRE-AQUECIMENTO E VERIFICACAO TERMICA COM CONTAGENS REAIS}
Gaq12_n = G_aq12/(1 [kg/m^2-s])
Gam12_n = G_am_ev12/(1 [kg/m^2-s])
Gaf12_n = G_af12/(1 [kg/m^2-s])
h_aq12 = OTEC_h(Gaq12_n,Dw_num,muw_num,kw_num,cpw_num,beta_M_num)*1 [W/m^2-K]*Convert(W,kW)
h_am_pre12 = OTEC_h(Gam12_n,Da_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,beta_M_num)*1 [W/m^2-K]*Convert(W,kW)
kaf12_n = k_sw_U[2]/(1 [W/m-K])
cpaf12_n = cp_af_ef*Convert(kJ,J)/(1 [J/kg-K])
h_af12 = OTEC_h(Gaf12_n,Dw_num,muCo12_n,kaf12_n,cpaf12_n,beta_M_num)*1 [W/m^2-K]*Convert(W,kW)
U_pre12 = 1/(1/h_aq12+R_placa+Rf_aq_pre+Rf_am_pre+1/h_am_pre12)
rA_pre12 = C_pre_U*Convert(J,kJ)/U_pre12
A_ev_req12 = m_am12*(rA_boil_ev+rA_pre12)
A_cond_req12 = m_am12*rA_cond
F_area_ev12 = A_ev_inst12/A_ev_req12
F_area_cond12 = A_cond_inst12/A_cond_req12
Margem_A_ev12 = A_ev_inst12-A_ev_req12
Margem_A_cond12 = A_cond_inst12-A_cond_req12
F_lim_boil12 = U_boil_ev*(1/h_aq12+R_placa)
F_lim_cond12 = U_phase_cond*(1/h_af12+R_placa)

{80. PERDAS DOS CANAIS COM CONTAGENS INTEIRAS}
Re_aq12 = G_aq12*Dh_ag/mu_aq
Re_af12 = G_af12*Dh_ag/mu_af
fD_aq12 = 4*OTEC_fF(Re_aq12,beta_M_num)
fD_af12 = 4*OTEC_fF(Re_af12,beta_M_num)
DP_canais_ev12 = fD_aq12*(L_placa/Dh_ag)*G_aq12^2/(2*rho_aq_med)*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_canais_cond12 = fD_af12*(L_placa/Dh_ag)*G_af12^2/(2*rho_af_med)*Convert(Pa,kPa)
P_canais_ev12 = DP_canais_ev12*(m_am12*r_m_aq/rho_aq_med)/eta_aq_tubo
P_canais_cond12 = DP_canais_cond12*(m_am12*r_m_af/rho_af_med)/eta_af_tubo

{81. FECHO ELECTRICO - A META CONTINUA A SER 10000 kW}
P_rede_af12 = OTEC_Prede11(m12_n,rvC10_n,N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,rhC10_n,muC10_n,epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo,K_af11)*1 [kW]
P_rede_aq12 = OTEC_Prede11(m12_n,rvH10_n,N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,rhH10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_aq11)*1 [kW]
P_col_af12 = DP_col_af11*Vdot_af12/eta_af_tubo
P_col_aq12 = DP_col_aq11*Vdot_aq12/eta_aq_tubo
P_b_am12 = m_am12*w_b_am_el
P_ger12 = m_am12*w_ger
P_consumos12 = P_b_am12+P_canais_ev12+P_canais_cond12+P_rede_af12+P_rede_aq12+P_col_af12+P_col_aq12
P_liq12 = P_ger12-P_consumos12
R_meta12 = P_liq12-P_liq_meta
R_meta_rel12 = ABS(R_meta12)/P_liq_meta
Fraccao_consumos12 = P_consumos12/P_ger12
R_placas12 = Np_total12-Np_act_total12-Np_term_total12
R_Pev12 = OTEC_Pcan12(m12_n,rmE12_n,Nc_ev12,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhE12_n,muE12_n,beta_M_num,eta_aq_tubo)*1 [kW]-P_canais_ev12
R_Pcond12 = OTEC_Pcan12(m12_n,rmC12_n,Nc_cond12,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhCo12_n,muCo12_n,beta_M_num,eta_af_tubo)*1 [kW]-P_canais_cond12
Controlo12 = OTEC_check12(F_area_ev12,F_area_cond12,Margem_v_af_tubo12/(1 [m/s]),Margem_v_aq_tubo12/(1 [m/s]),Margem_v_ev12/(1 [m/s]),Margem_v_cond12/(1 [m/s]),R_meta12/(1 [kW]))
Fornecedor_confirmado12 = 0
Fecho_definitivo12 = 0

$VarInfo A_pre_req13 Units='m^2'
$VarInfo U_boil_min13 Units='kW/m^2-K'

$VarInfo P_liq_fixo13[1] Units='kW'
$VarInfo Desvio_meta_fixo13[1] Units='kW'
$VarInfo fU_stress13[1] Units='-'
$VarInfo A_ev_stress13[1] Units='m^2'
$VarInfo A_co_stress13[1] Units='m^2'
$VarInfo F_ev_stress13[1] Units='-'
$VarInfo F_co_stress13[1] Units='-'
$VarInfo expo_sens13[2] Units='-'
$VarInfo DP_col_sens13[2] Units='kPa'
$VarInfo P_col_sens13[2] Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_liq_fixo13[2] Units='kW'
$VarInfo Desvio_meta_fixo13[2] Units='kW'
$VarInfo fU_stress13[2] Units='-'
$VarInfo A_ev_stress13[2] Units='m^2'
$VarInfo A_co_stress13[2] Units='m^2'
$VarInfo F_ev_stress13[2] Units='-'
$VarInfo F_co_stress13[2] Units='-'
$VarInfo expo_sens13[3] Units='-'
$VarInfo DP_col_sens13[3] Units='kPa'
$VarInfo P_col_sens13[3] Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_liq_fixo13[3] Units='kW'
$VarInfo Desvio_meta_fixo13[3] Units='kW'
$VarInfo fU_stress13[3] Units='-'
$VarInfo A_ev_stress13[3] Units='m^2'
$VarInfo A_co_stress13[3] Units='m^2'
$VarInfo F_ev_stress13[3] Units='-'
$VarInfo F_co_stress13[3] Units='-'

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 13 - MARGENS E SENSIBILIDADE}

{82. MINIMOS DE U PARA AS AREAS ACTUAIS E O PONTO NOMINAL}
A_pre_req13 = m_am12*rA_pre12
U_boil_min13 = m_am12*q_boil_ev/((A_ev_inst12-A_pre_req13)*DTlm_boil)
U_cond_min13 = m_am12*q_cond/(A_cond_inst12*DTlm_cond)
Reduc_Uboil_adm13 = 1-U_boil_min13/U_boil_ev
Reduc_Ucond_adm13 = 1-U_cond_min13/U_phase_cond
Rextra_cond_adm13 = 1/U_cond_min13-1/U_phase_cond
h_am_boil_exig13 = 1/(1/U_boil_ev-1/h_aq12-R_placa)
h_am_cond_exig13 = 1/(1/U_phase_cond-1/h_af12-R_placa)

{83. CAUDAL MAXIMO TERMICAMENTE COMPATIVEL COM OS MODULOS ACTUAIS}
Ainst_ev13_n = A_ev_inst12/(1 [m^2])
m_lim_ev13_n = OTEC_mA13(Ainst_ev13_n,m12_n,mmax10_n,Nc_ev12,rm_num,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,muw_num,kw_num,cpw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,beta_M_num,Rb_num,Cpre_num)
m_lim_ev13 = m_lim_ev13_n*1 [kg/s]
m_lim_cond13 = A_cond_inst12/rA_cond
m_lim13 = MIN(m_lim_ev13,m_lim_cond13,m_am_cap_cenario)
m_lim13_n = m_lim13/(1 [kg/s])
Vdot_af_lim13 = m_lim13*r_V_af
Vdot_aq_lim13 = m_lim13*r_V_aq
P_canais_ev_lim13 = OTEC_Pcan12(m_lim13_n,rmE12_n,Nc_ev12,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhE12_n,muE12_n,beta_M_num,eta_aq_tubo)*1 [kW]
P_canais_co_lim13 = OTEC_Pcan12(m_lim13_n,rmC12_n,Nc_cond12,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhCo12_n,muCo12_n,beta_M_num,eta_af_tubo)*1 [kW]
P_rede_af_lim13 = OTEC_Prede11(m_lim13_n,rvC10_n,N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,rhC10_n,muC10_n,epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo,K_af11)*1 [kW]
P_rede_aq_lim13 = OTEC_Prede11(m_lim13_n,rvH10_n,N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,rhH10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_aq11)*1 [kW]
P_disponivel_lim13 = m_lim13*w_sem_canais12-P_canais_ev_lim13-P_canais_co_lim13-P_rede_af_lim13-P_rede_aq_lim13
P_omiss_adm13 = P_disponivel_lim13-P_liq_meta
Frac_meta_omiss13 = P_omiss_adm13/P_liq_meta
DP_extra_so_af13 = P_omiss_adm13*eta_af_tubo/Vdot_af_lim13
DP_extra_so_aq13 = P_omiss_adm13*eta_aq_tubo/Vdot_aq_lim13

{84. SENSIBILIDADE AO PERFIL DE DENSIDADE - CAUDAL E MODULOS FIXOS}
expo_sens13[1] = 0.5
expo_sens13[2] = 1
expo_sens13[3] = 2
Duplicate s13=1,3
  DP_col_sens13[s13] = DP_col_envelope11*OTEC_colfrac11(expo_sens13[s13])
  P_col_sens13[s13] = DP_col_sens13[s13]*Vdot_af12/eta_af_tubo
  P_liq_fixo13[s13] = P_liq12+P_col_af12-P_col_sens13[s13]
  Desvio_meta_fixo13[s13] = P_liq_fixo13[s13]-P_liq_meta
End

{85. TESTE DE CAPACIDADE A REDUCAO DE U - CAUDAL FIXO}
fU_stress13[1] = 0.99
fU_stress13[2] = 0.95
fU_stress13[3] = 0.90
Duplicate t13=1,3
  A_ev_stress13[t13] = A_pre_req13+m_am12*rA_boil_ev/fU_stress13[t13]
  A_co_stress13[t13] = m_am12*rA_cond/fU_stress13[t13]
  F_ev_stress13[t13] = A_ev_inst12/A_ev_stress13[t13]
  F_co_stress13[t13] = A_cond_inst12/A_co_stress13[t13]
End

{86. APLICABILIDADE BIFASICA ACTUALIZADA A GEOMETRIA DISCRETA}
Razao_G_Tao_min13 = G_am_cond12/G_Tao_min
Fora_G_Tao13 = IF(G_am_cond12,G_Tao_min,1,0,IF(G_am_cond12,G_Tao_max,0,0,1))
Excesso_P_Koyama13 = (P_ev-P_Koyama_max)/P_Koyama_max
U_bifasico_validado13 = 0
Correlacao_DP_NH3_valid13 = 0
Perfil_vertical_real13 = Perfil_medido_disponivel11
Inventario_perdas_completo13 = 0
Fecho_definitivo13 = 0

$VarInfo P_liq_nom13 Units='kW'
$VarInfo R_nom13 Units='kW'
P_liq_nom13 = P_liq12
R_nom13 = P_liq_nom13-P_liq_meta

$VarInfo A_ev_inst14 Units='m^2'

$VarInfo P_omiss_total_adm14 Units='kW'
$VarInfo P_saldo_apos_reserva14 Units='kW'

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 14 - CENARIO CONSOLIDADO PARA SIMULACAO}

{87. PARAMETROS EXPLICITOS DO CENARIO}
fU_bif_proj14 = 0.90
U_boil14 = fU_bif_proj14*U_boil_ev
U_cond14 = fU_bif_proj14*U_phase_cond
P_reserva14 = 500 [kW]
Frac_reserva_meta14 = P_reserva14/P_liq_meta
P_alvo_antes_reserva14 = P_liq_meta+P_reserva14
P_alvo14_n = P_alvo_antes_reserva14/(1 [kW])

{88. CONFIGURACAO DISCRETA FIXADA PARA ESTE CASO}
Nmod_ev14 = 108
Nmod_cond14 = 108
Nc_ev14 = Nmod_ev14*Nc_agua_mod12
Nc_cond14 = Nmod_cond14*Nc_agua_mod12
Nc_am_ev14 = Nmod_ev14*Nc_am_mod12
Nc_am_cond14 = Nmod_cond14*Nc_am_mod12
Np_ev14 = Nmod_ev14*Np_pacote_mod12
Np_cond14 = Nmod_cond14*Np_pacote_mod12
Np_total14 = Np_ev14+Np_cond14
Np_activas14 = (Nmod_ev14+Nmod_cond14)*Np_act_mod12
Np_terminais14 = (Nmod_ev14+Nmod_cond14)*Np_term_mod12
A_ev_inst14 = Nmod_ev14*A_mod12
A_cond_inst14 = Nmod_cond14*A_mod12

{89. CAUDAL REAJUSTADO - PERDAS CALCULADAS COM OS NOVOS CANAIS}
m14_n = OTEC_mrob14(P_alvo14_n,w12_n,mmax10_n,rvC10_n,rvH10_n,N_CWP_cenario,N_aq_cenario,aC10_n,aH10_n,dC10_n,dH10_n,lC10_n,lH10_n,rhC10_n,rhH10_n,muC10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_CWP,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_af_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_af11,K_aq11,Nc_ev14,Nc_cond14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rmE12_n,rmC12_n,rhE12_n,rhCo12_n,muE12_n,muCo12_n,beta_M_num)
m_am14 = m14_n*1 [kg/s]
m_aq14 = m_am14*r_m_aq
m_af14 = m_am14*r_m_af
Vdot_aq14 = m_am14*r_V_aq
Vdot_af14 = m_am14*r_V_af
v_aq_tubo14 = Vdot_aq14/(N_aq_cenario*A_aq_tubo)
v_af_tubo14 = Vdot_af14/(N_CWP_cenario*A_CWP)
G_aq14 = m_aq14/(Nc_ev14*A_canal_ag)
G_af14 = m_af14/(Nc_cond14*A_canal_ag)
G_am_ev14 = m_am14/(Nc_am_ev14*A_canal_am)
G_am_cond14 = m_am14/(Nc_am_cond14*A_canal_am)
v_aq_canal14 = G_aq14/rho_aq_min
v_af_canal14 = G_af14/rho_af_min
Margem_v_aq_tubo14 = v_tubo_max-v_aq_tubo14
Margem_v_af_tubo14 = v_tubo_max-v_af_tubo14
Margem_v_ev14 = v_canal_ref-v_aq_canal14
Margem_v_cond14 = v_canal_ref-v_af_canal14

{90. U DE PRE-AQUECIMENTO E AREAS REQUERIDAS DO CENARIO}
Gaq14_n = G_aq14/(1 [kg/m^2-s])
Gaf14_n = G_af14/(1 [kg/m^2-s])
Gam14_n = G_am_ev14/(1 [kg/m^2-s])
h_aq14 = OTEC_h(Gaq14_n,Dw_num,muw_num,kw_num,cpw_num,beta_M_num)*1 [W/m^2-K]*Convert(W,kW)
h_af14 = OTEC_h(Gaf14_n,Dw_num,muCo12_n,kaf12_n,cpaf12_n,beta_M_num)*1 [W/m^2-K]*Convert(W,kW)
h_am_pre14 = OTEC_h(Gam14_n,Da_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,beta_M_num)*1 [W/m^2-K]*Convert(W,kW)
U_pre14 = 1/(1/h_aq14+R_placa+Rf_aq_pre+Rf_am_pre+1/h_am_pre14)
rA_pre14 = C_pre_U*Convert(J,kJ)/U_pre14
rA_boil14 = q_boil_ev/(U_boil14*DTlm_boil)
rA_cond14 = q_cond/(U_cond14*DTlm_cond)
A_pre_req14 = m_am14*rA_pre14
A_boil_req14 = m_am14*rA_boil14
A_ev_req14 = A_pre_req14+A_boil_req14
A_cond_req14 = m_am14*rA_cond14
F_area_ev14 = A_ev_inst14/A_ev_req14
F_area_cond14 = A_cond_inst14/A_cond_req14
Margem_A_ev14 = A_ev_inst14-A_ev_req14
Margem_A_cond14 = A_cond_inst14-A_cond_req14
F_lim_boil14 = U_boil14*(1/h_aq14+R_placa)
F_lim_cond14 = U_cond14*(1/h_af14+R_placa)
h_am_boil_exig14 = 1/(1/U_boil14-1/h_aq14-R_placa)
h_am_cond_exig14 = 1/(1/U_cond14-1/h_af14-R_placa)

Q_ev14 = m_am14*q_ev
Q_cond14 = m_am14*q_cond

{91. PERDAS DOS CANAIS NO NOVO PONTO}
Re_aq14 = G_aq14*Dh_ag/mu_aq
Re_af14 = G_af14*Dh_ag/mu_af
fD_aq14 = 4*OTEC_fF(Re_aq14,beta_M_num)
fD_af14 = 4*OTEC_fF(Re_af14,beta_M_num)
DP_ev_canais14 = fD_aq14*(L_placa/Dh_ag)*G_aq14^2/(2*rho_aq_med)*Convert(Pa,kPa)
DP_cond_canais14 = fD_af14*(L_placa/Dh_ag)*G_af14^2/(2*rho_af_med)*Convert(Pa,kPa)
P_ev_canais14 = DP_ev_canais14*(m_aq14/rho_aq_med)/eta_aq_tubo
P_cond_canais14 = DP_cond_canais14*(m_af14/rho_af_med)/eta_af_tubo

{92. BALANCO DE POTENCIA - META DE EXPORTACAO APOS RESERVA}
P_rede_af14 = OTEC_Prede11(m14_n,rvC10_n,N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,rhC10_n,muC10_n,epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo,K_af11)*1 [kW]
P_rede_aq14 = OTEC_Prede11(m14_n,rvH10_n,N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,rhH10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_aq11)*1 [kW]
P_col_af14 = DP_col_af11*Vdot_af14/eta_af_tubo
P_col_aq14 = DP_col_aq11*Vdot_aq14/eta_aq_tubo
P_b_am14 = m_am14*w_b_am_el
P_ger14 = m_am14*w_ger
P_consumos_modelados14 = P_b_am14+P_ev_canais14+P_cond_canais14+P_rede_af14+P_rede_aq14+P_col_af14+P_col_aq14
P_apos_perdas_modeladas14 = P_ger14-P_consumos_modelados14
P_liq14 = P_apos_perdas_modeladas14-P_reserva14
R_meta14 = P_liq14-P_liq_meta
R_meta_rel14 = ABS(R_meta14)/P_liq_meta
Fraccao_consumos14 = (P_consumos_modelados14+P_reserva14)/P_ger14
R_placas14 = Np_total14-Np_activas14-Np_terminais14
R_Pev14 = OTEC_Pcan12(m14_n,rmE12_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhE12_n,muE12_n,beta_M_num,eta_aq_tubo)*1 [kW]-P_ev_canais14
R_Pcond14 = OTEC_Pcan12(m14_n,rmC12_n,Nc_cond14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhCo12_n,muCo12_n,beta_M_num,eta_af_tubo)*1 [kW]-P_cond_canais14
Controlo14 = OTEC_check12(F_area_ev14,F_area_cond14,Margem_v_af_tubo14/(1 [m/s]),Margem_v_aq_tubo14/(1 [m/s]),Margem_v_ev14/(1 [m/s]),Margem_v_cond14/(1 [m/s]),R_meta14/(1 [kW]))

{93. VERIFICACAO DE CAPACIDADE DESTA CONFIGURACAO}
Ainst_ev14_n = A_ev_inst14/(1 [m^2])
Rb14_n = rA_boil14/(1 [m^2-s/kg])
m_lim_ev14_n = OTEC_mA13(Ainst_ev14_n,m14_n,mmax10_n,Nc_ev14,rm_num,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,muw_num,kw_num,cpw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,beta_M_num,Rb14_n,Cpre_num)
m_lim_ev14 = m_lim_ev14_n*1 [kg/s]
m_lim_cond14 = A_cond_inst14/rA_cond14
m_lim14 = MIN(m_lim_ev14,m_lim_cond14,m_am_cap_cenario)
m_lim14_n = m_lim14/(1 [kg/s])
P_ev_lim14 = OTEC_Pcan12(m_lim14_n,rmE12_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhE12_n,muE12_n,beta_M_num,eta_aq_tubo)*1 [kW]
P_cond_lim14 = OTEC_Pcan12(m_lim14_n,rmC12_n,Nc_cond14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,rhCo12_n,muCo12_n,beta_M_num,eta_af_tubo)*1 [kW]
P_rede_af_lim14 = OTEC_Prede11(m_lim14_n,rvC10_n,N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,rhC10_n,muC10_n,epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo,K_af11)*1 [kW]
P_rede_aq_lim14 = OTEC_Prede11(m_lim14_n,rvH10_n,N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,rhH10_n,muH10_n,epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_aq11)*1 [kW]
P_omiss_total_adm14 = m_lim14*w_sem_canais12-P_ev_lim14-P_cond_lim14-P_rede_af_lim14-P_rede_aq_lim14-P_liq_meta
P_saldo_apos_reserva14 = P_omiss_total_adm14-P_reserva14

{94. INDICADORES DE VALIDACAO}
U_bifasico_validado14 = 0
Perfil_real_disponivel14 = Perfil_medido_disponivel11
Fornecedor_confirmado14 = 0
Inventario_perdas_completo14 = 0
Fecho_definitivo14 = 0
Cenario_pronto14 = Controlo14

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 15 - OPERACAO COM INSTALACAO FIXA}

{95. ENTRADAS FIXAS DO EQUIPAMENTO}
Mref15_n = m_am14/(1 [kg/s])
MWaq15_n = m_aq14/(1 [kg/s])
MWaf15_n = m_af14/(1 [kg/s])
AE15_n = A_ev_inst14/(1 [m^2])
AC15_n = A_cond_inst14/(1 [m^2])
UB15_n = U_boil14/(1 [kW/m^2-K])
UC15_n = U_cond14/(1 [kW/m^2-K])
QE15_n = q_ev/(1 [kJ/kg])
QB15_n = q_boil_ev/(1 [kJ/kg])
QP15_n = q_pre_ev/(1 [kJ/kg])
QC15_n = q_cond/(1 [kJ/kg])
TE15_n = (T_ev-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TC15_n = (T_cond-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
PE15_n = DTpinch_ev/(1 [DELTAC])
PC15_n = DTpinch_cond/(1 [DELTAC])
P_agua_fixa15 = P_ev_canais14+P_cond_canais14+P_rede_af14+P_rede_aq14+P_col_af14+P_col_aq14
TA15_n[1] = (T_am_pre[1]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[2] = (T_am_pre[2]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[3] = (T_am_pre[3]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[4] = (T_am_pre[4]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[5] = (T_am_pre[5]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[6] = (T_am_pre[6]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[7] = (T_am_pre[7]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[8] = (T_am_pre[8]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[9] = (T_am_pre[9]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[10] = (T_am_pre[10]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
TA15_n[11] = (T_am_pre[11]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])

{96. CENARIOS DE TEMPERATURA - NAO SAO SERIES OBSERVADAS}
Taq_in15[1] = 27.87 [C]

{97. CAPACIDADE TERMICA; CAUDAL DE AMONIA COMO RESULTADO}
Duplicate k15=1,13
  TH15_n[k15] = (Taq_in15[k15]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  TL15_n[k15] = (Taf_in15[k15]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  mcap_ev15_n[k15] = OTEC_mEv15(Mref15_n,TH15_n[k15],MWaq15_n,AE15_n,UB15_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,beta_M_num,SP_aq,kw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,QE15_n,QB15_n,QP15_n,TE15_n,PE15_n,TA15_n[1],TA15_n[2],TA15_n[3],TA15_n[4],TA15_n[5],TA15_n[6],TA15_n[7],TA15_n[8],TA15_n[9],TA15_n[10],TA15_n[11])
  mcap_co15_n[k15] = OTEC_mCo15(Mref15_n,TL15_n[k15],MWaf15_n,AC15_n,UC15_n,QC15_n,TC15_n,PC15_n)
  mop15_n[k15] = MIN(Mref15_n,mcap_ev15_n[k15],mcap_co15_n[k15])
  m_am_op15[k15] = mop15_n[k15]*1 [kg/s]
  Carga_rel15[k15] = mop15_n[k15]/Mref15_n
  Uso_ev15[k15] = OTEC_ev15(1,mop15_n[k15],TH15_n[k15],MWaq15_n,AE15_n,UB15_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,beta_M_num,SP_aq,kw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,QE15_n,QB15_n,QP15_n,TE15_n,PE15_n,TA15_n[1],TA15_n[2],TA15_n[3],TA15_n[4],TA15_n[5],TA15_n[6],TA15_n[7],TA15_n[8],TA15_n[9],TA15_n[10],TA15_n[11])
  Uso_co15[k15] = OTEC_co15(1,mop15_n[k15],TL15_n[k15],MWaf15_n,AC15_n,UC15_n,QC15_n,TC15_n,PC15_n)
  A_ev_req15[k15] = OTEC_ev15(2,mop15_n[k15],TH15_n[k15],MWaq15_n,AE15_n,UB15_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,beta_M_num,SP_aq,kw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,QE15_n,QB15_n,QP15_n,TE15_n,PE15_n,TA15_n[1],TA15_n[2],TA15_n[3],TA15_n[4],TA15_n[5],TA15_n[6],TA15_n[7],TA15_n[8],TA15_n[9],TA15_n[10],TA15_n[11])*1 [m^2]
  A_co_req15[k15] = OTEC_co15(2,mop15_n[k15],TL15_n[k15],MWaf15_n,AC15_n,UC15_n,QC15_n,TC15_n,PC15_n)*1 [m^2]
  DTmin_ev15[k15] = OTEC_ev15(3,mop15_n[k15],TH15_n[k15],MWaq15_n,AE15_n,UB15_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,beta_M_num,SP_aq,kw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,QE15_n,QB15_n,QP15_n,TE15_n,PE15_n,TA15_n[1],TA15_n[2],TA15_n[3],TA15_n[4],TA15_n[5],TA15_n[6],TA15_n[7],TA15_n[8],TA15_n[9],TA15_n[10],TA15_n[11])*1 [DELTAC]
  DTmin_co15[k15] = OTEC_co15(3,mop15_n[k15],TL15_n[k15],MWaf15_n,AC15_n,UC15_n,QC15_n,TC15_n,PC15_n)*1 [DELTAC]
  U_pre_op15[k15] = OTEC_ev15(4,mop15_n[k15],TH15_n[k15],MWaq15_n,AE15_n,UB15_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,beta_M_num,SP_aq,kw_num,mua_num,ka_num,cpa_num,Rt_num,QE15_n,QB15_n,QP15_n,TE15_n,PE15_n,TA15_n[1],TA15_n[2],TA15_n[3],TA15_n[4],TA15_n[5],TA15_n[6],TA15_n[7],TA15_n[8],TA15_n[9],TA15_n[10],TA15_n[11])*1 [kW/m^2-K]
  Hout_aq15_n[k15] = OTEC_hsw15(TH15_n[k15],1)-mop15_n[k15]*QE15_n/MWaq15_n
  Hout_af15_n[k15] = OTEC_hsw15(TL15_n[k15],2)+mop15_n[k15]*QC15_n/MWaf15_n
  Tout_aq15_n[k15] = OTEC_Tsw15(Hout_aq15_n[k15],1)
  Tout_af15_n[k15] = OTEC_Tsw15(Hout_af15_n[k15],2)
  Taq_out15[k15] = 0 [C]+Tout_aq15_n[k15]*1 [DELTAC]
  Taf_out15[k15] = 0 [C]+Tout_af15_n[k15]*1 [DELTAC]
  R_Qaq15[k15] = (mop15_n[k15]*QE15_n-MWaq15_n*(OTEC_hsw15(TH15_n[k15],1)-OTEC_hsw15(Tout_aq15_n[k15],1)))*1 [kW]
  R_Qaf15[k15] = (mop15_n[k15]*QC15_n-MWaf15_n*(OTEC_hsw15(Tout_af15_n[k15],2)-OTEC_hsw15(TL15_n[k15],2)))*1 [kW]

  {98. PARCELA ELECTRICA DA AGUA FIXA PARA ISOLAR O EFEITO TERMICO}
  {99. BALANCO EM OPERACAO - POTENCIA NAO IMPOSTA}
  P_ger15[k15] = m_am_op15[k15]*w_ger
  P_b_am15[k15] = m_am_op15[k15]*w_b_am_el
  P_consumos15[k15] = P_b_am15[k15]+P_agua_fixa15
  P_liq15[k15] = P_ger15[k15]-P_consumos15[k15]-P_reserva14
  Margem_meta15[k15] = P_liq15[k15]-P_liq_meta
  Cumpre_meta15[k15] = IF(P_liq15[k15]+0.01 [kW],P_liq_meta,0,1,1)
  Balanco_positivo15[k15] = IF(P_liq15[k15],0 [kW],0,0,1)
  Excesso_uso15[k15] = MAX(Uso_ev15[k15]-1,Uso_co15[k15]-1,0)
End

{100. CONTROLO DA REFERENCIA}
R_ref_p15 = P_liq15[1]-P_liq14
R_ref_m15 = m_am_op15[1]-m_am14
R_ref_rel15 = ABS(R_ref_p15)/P_liq_meta

$VarInfo D_comp16[5] Units='kW'
$VarInfo Fraccao_D16[5] Units='-'
$VarInfo D_comp16[6] Units='kW'
$VarInfo Fraccao_D16[6] Units='-'

{OTEC NACALA - PARTE 16 - ANALISE EXERGETICA DO NUCLEO TERMICO}

{101. REFERENCIA AMBIENTAL E EXERGIA ESPECIFICA DA AMONIA}
T0K16 = ConvertTemp(C,K,T0)
T0num16 = (T0-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
h0_am16 = Enthalpy(Ammonia,T=T0,P=P0)
s0_am16 = Entropy(Ammonia,T=T0,P=P0)
Duplicate st16=1,4
  ex_am16[st16] = h[st16]-h0_am16-T0K16*(s[st16]-s0_am16)
End
h0_aq16 = OTEC_hsw15(T0num16,1)*1 [kJ/kg]
h0_af16 = OTEC_hsw15(T0num16,2)*1 [kJ/kg]
s0_aq16 = OTEC_ssw16(T0num16,1)*1 [kJ/kg-K]
s0_af16 = OTEC_ssw16(T0num16,2)*1 [kJ/kg-K]
R_SA16 = MAX(ABS((SA_aq-34.986434046 [g/kg])/(1 [g/kg])),ABS((SA_af-35.005680627 [g/kg])/(1 [g/kg])))

{102. TREZE CASOS COM A FRONTEIRA TERMICA IDENTICA}
Duplicate ex16=1,13
  TinH16_n[ex16] = (Taq_in15[ex16]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  ToutH16_n[ex16] = (Taq_out15[ex16]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  TinC16_n[ex16] = (Taf_in15[ex16]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  ToutC16_n[ex16] = (Taf_out15[ex16]-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])
  hHin16[ex16] = OTEC_hsw15(TinH16_n[ex16],1)*1 [kJ/kg]
  hHout16[ex16] = OTEC_hsw15(ToutH16_n[ex16],1)*1 [kJ/kg]
  hCin16[ex16] = OTEC_hsw15(TinC16_n[ex16],2)*1 [kJ/kg]
  hCout16[ex16] = OTEC_hsw15(ToutC16_n[ex16],2)*1 [kJ/kg]
  sHin16[ex16] = OTEC_ssw16(TinH16_n[ex16],1)*1 [kJ/kg-K]
  sHout16[ex16] = OTEC_ssw16(ToutH16_n[ex16],1)*1 [kJ/kg-K]
  sCin16[ex16] = OTEC_ssw16(TinC16_n[ex16],2)*1 [kJ/kg-K]
  sCout16[ex16] = OTEC_ssw16(ToutC16_n[ex16],2)*1 [kJ/kg-K]
  eHin16[ex16] = hHin16[ex16]-h0_aq16-T0K16*(sHin16[ex16]-s0_aq16)
  eHout16[ex16] = hHout16[ex16]-h0_aq16-T0K16*(sHout16[ex16]-s0_aq16)
  eCin16[ex16] = hCin16[ex16]-h0_af16-T0K16*(sCin16[ex16]-s0_af16)
  eCout16[ex16] = hCout16[ex16]-h0_af16-T0K16*(sCout16[ex16]-s0_af16)
  Ex_in16[ex16] = m_aq14*eHin16[ex16]+m_af14*eCin16[ex16]
  Ex_out16[ex16] = m_aq14*eHout16[ex16]+m_af14*eCout16[ex16]
  Ex_usada16[ex16] = Ex_in16[ex16]-Ex_out16[ex16]

  {103. GERACAO DE ENTROPIA E DESTRUICOES INTERNAS}
  Sg_t16[ex16] = m_am_op15[ex16]*(s[4]-s[3])
  Sg_b16[ex16] = m_am_op15[ex16]*(s[2]-s[1])
  Sg_ev16[ex16] = m_am_op15[ex16]*(s[3]-s[2])+m_aq14*(sHout16[ex16]-sHin16[ex16])
  Sg_co16[ex16] = m_am_op15[ex16]*(s[1]-s[4])+m_af14*(sCout16[ex16]-sCin16[ex16])
  D_t16[ex16] = T0K16*Sg_t16[ex16]
  D_b16[ex16] = T0K16*Sg_b16[ex16]
  D_ev16[ex16] = T0K16*Sg_ev16[ex16]
  D_co16[ex16] = T0K16*Sg_co16[ex16]
  P_t_eixo16[ex16] = m_am_op15[ex16]*w_t
  P_b_eixo16[ex16] = m_am_op15[ex16]*w_b_am
  
  D_g16[ex16] = P_t_eixo16[ex16]-P_ger15[ex16]
  D_m16[ex16] = P_b_am15[ex16]-P_b_eixo16[ex16]
  D_total16[ex16] = D_t16[ex16]+D_b16[ex16]+D_ev16[ex16]+D_co16[ex16]+D_g16[ex16]+D_m16[ex16]
  P_core16[ex16] = P_ger15[ex16]-P_b_am15[ex16]

  {104. INDICADORES COM DUAS FRONTEIRAS EXPLICITAS}
  Eta_depl16[ex16] = P_core16[ex16]/Ex_usada16[ex16]
  Eta_in16[ex16] = P_core16[ex16]/Ex_in16[ex16]
  R_ex16[ex16] = Ex_in16[ex16]-Ex_out16[ex16]-P_core16[ex16]-D_total16[ex16]
  R_ex_rel16[ex16] = R_ex16[ex16]/Ex_usada16[ex16]
  R_el16[ex16] = P_core16[ex16]-P_agua_fixa15-P_reserva14-P_liq15[ex16]
  R_el_rel16[ex16] = R_el16[ex16]/P_liq_meta
  R_ev16[ex16] = m_aq14*(eHin16[ex16]-eHout16[ex16])+m_am_op15[ex16]*(ex_am16[2]-ex_am16[3])-D_ev16[ex16]
  R_co16[ex16] = m_af14*(eCin16[ex16]-eCout16[ex16])+m_am_op15[ex16]*(ex_am16[4]-ex_am16[1])-D_co16[ex16]
  Dmin16[ex16] = MIN(D_t16[ex16],D_b16[ex16],D_ev16[ex16],D_co16[ex16],D_g16[ex16],D_m16[ex16])
  emin16[ex16] = MIN(eHin16[ex16],eHout16[ex16],eCin16[ex16],eCout16[ex16],ex_am16[1..4])
  Val_ex16[ex16] = OTEC_valEx16(Dmin16[ex16]/(1 [kW]),Ex_in16[ex16]/(1 [kW]),Ex_usada16[ex16]/(1 [kW]),emin16[ex16]/(1 [kJ/kg]),R_ex_rel16[ex16],R_el_rel16[ex16],R_SA16)
End

{105. RESULTADOS ESCALARES DA REFERENCIA PARA CONFERENCIA}
D_turb16 = D_t16[1]
D_bomba_am16 = D_b16[1]
D_evap16 = D_ev16[1]
D_cond16 = D_co16[1]
D_gerador16 = D_g16[1]
D_motor_am16 = D_m16[1]
D_nucleo16 = D_total16[1]
Ex_in_ref16 = Ex_in16[1]
Ex_saidas_ref16 = Ex_out16[1]
Ex_usada_ref16 = Ex_usada16[1]
P_core_ref16 = P_core16[1]
Eta_II_depl_ref16 = Eta_depl16[1]
Eta_II_in_ref16 = Eta_in16[1]
R_ex_ref16 = R_ex16[1]
R_ex_rel_ref16 = R_ex_rel16[1]
R_el_ref16 = R_el16[1]
Todos_ex_validos16 = MIN(Val_ex16[1..13])

{106. PARTICIPACAO NAS DESTRUICOES DO NUCLEO}
D_comp16[1] = D_turb16

D_comp16[4] = D_cond16
D_comp16[5] = D_gerador16
D_comp16[6] = D_motor_am16
Duplicate dc16=1,6
  Fraccao_D16[dc16] = D_comp16[dc16]/D_nucleo16
End
R_fraccoes16 = SUM(Fraccao_D16[1..6])-1
Ex_hidraulica_completa16 = 0
Mistura_descarga_modelada16 = 0

{PARTE 17 - COMPARACAO NO PAR DE REFERENCIA
 Te/Tc: temperaturas de saturacao; MH/MC: caudais de agua quente/fria.
 Modos 1=MH/MC nominais; 2=MH/Tc; 3=Te/MC; 4=Te/Tc.
 Equipamento e NH3 nominais. Ramos activos: area do evaporador e pinch
 do condensador, conforme ensaio do modo 4. Res_Val verifica os demais
 limites. Aplicacao a outros pares requer verificar os ramos activos.
 Seleccao por menor auxiliar admissivel, empate 0.001 kW: menor indice.
 Modo seleccionado 0 significa nenhum candidato admissivel.
 Ensaio de referencia; nao e serie mensal nem optimizacao global.}
Taq_in17B = 27.87 [C]
Taf_in17B = 5.89 [C]

{MODO 1}
{107. ENTRADAS DE OPERACAO}
Modo17B[1] = 1
m_am17B[1] = m_am14

{108. QUATRO COMBINACOES DE CONTROLO; EQUACOES NORMALIZADAS}
Fixa_MH17B[1] = IF(Modo17B[1],2.5,1,0,0)
Fixa_MC17B[1] = IF(Modo17B[1],1.5,1,0,0)+IF(Modo17B[1],3,0,1,0)
m_aq17B[1] = m_aq14
m_af17B[1] = m_af14

{109. CICLO NH3 SEQUENCIAL}
P_ev17B[1] = OTEC_cy17(1,(TEs17_1-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_1-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1)*1 [kPa]

{111. BOMBEAMENTO COM CAUDAIS RECALCULADOS}
RHOH17B_n[2] = OTEC_rhosw15(TH17B_n[2],1)
RHOC17B_n[2] = OTEC_rhosw15(TL17B_n[2],2)

MUC17B_n[2] = OTEC_muSW_tubo(TL17B_n[2],SP_af)
MUMH17B_n[2] = OTEC_muSW_tubo(TMH17B_n[2],SP_aq)
MUMC17B_n[2] = OTEC_muSW_tubo(TMC17B_n[2],SP_af)
P_tubo_aq17B[2] = OTEC_Prede11(MH17B_n[2],1/RHOH17B_n[2],N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,RHOH17B_n[2],MUH17B_n[2],epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_aq11)*1 [kW]
P_tubo_af17B[2] = OTEC_Prede11(MC17B_n[2],1/RHOC17B_n[2],N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,RHOC17B_n[2],MUC17B_n[2],epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo,K_af11)*1 [kW]
P_canal_aq17B[2] = OTEC_Pcan12(MH17B_n[2],1,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,RHOMH17B_n[2],MUMH17B_n[2],beta_M_num,eta_aq_tubo)*1 [kW]
P_canal_af17B[2] = OTEC_Pcan12(MC17B_n[2],1,Nc_cond14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,RHOMC17B_n[2],MUMC17B_n[2],beta_M_num,eta_af_tubo)*1 [kW]
DP_col17B[2] = g11*(RHOC17B_n[2]-RHOH17B_n[2])*1 [kg/m^3]*H_vertical_CWP*OTEC_colfrac11(expo_perfil11)*Convert(Pa,kPa)
P_col17B[2] = DP_col17B[2]*(m_af17B[2]/(RHOC17B_n[2]*1 [kg/m^3]))/eta_af_tubo
P_agua17B[2] = P_tubo_aq17B[2]+P_tubo_af17B[2]+P_canal_aq17B[2]+P_canal_af17B[2]+P_col17B[2]
P_ger17B[2] = m_am17B[2]*w_t17B[2]*eta_ger
P_b_am17B[2] = m_am17B[2]*w_b17B[2]/eta_m_am
P_aux17B[2] = P_agua17B[2]+P_b_am17B[2]
P_liq_antes_reserva17B[2] = P_ger17B[2]-P_aux17B[2]
P_liq17B[2] = P_liq_antes_reserva17B[2]-P_reserva14
Delta_P_ref17B[2] = P_liq17B[2]-P_liq14
Calor_ev17B[2] = m_am17B[2]*q_ev17B[2]
Calor_co17B[2] = m_am17B[2]*q_cond17B[2]
R_ciclo17B[2] = m_am17B[2]*(q_ev17B[2]-q_cond17B[2]-w_t17B[2]+w_b17B[2])
R_QH17B[2] = (MA17B_n[2]*QE17B_n[2]-MH17B_n[2]*(OTEC_hsw15(TH17B_n[2],1)-OTEC_hsw15(ToutH17B_n[2],1)))*1 [kW]

{112. ADMISSIBILIDADE SEPARADA DA CONVERGENCIA}

{112. ADMISSIBILIDADE SEPARADA DA CONVERGENCIA}

Admissivel17B[3] = IF(Margem_oper17B[3],(-0.00001),0,1,1)*IF(Erro_bal17B[3],0.001,1,1,0)

U_pre17B[4] = OTEC_ev15(4,MA17B_n[4],TH17B_n[4],MH17B_n[4],AE15_n,UB15_n,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Aa_num,Dw_num,Da_num,beta_M_num,SP_aq,kw_num,OTEC_cy17(21,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1),OTEC_cy17(22,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1),OTEC_cy17(23,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1),Rt_num,OTEC_cy17(13,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1),OTEC_cy17(15,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1),OTEC_cy17(14,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1),((TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])),PE15_n,OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,2),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,3),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,4),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,5),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,6),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,7),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,8),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,9),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,10),OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,11))*1 [kW/m^2-K]
HoutH17B_n[4] = OTEC_hsw15(TH17B_n[4],1)-MA17B_n[4]*QE17B_n[4]/MH17B_n[4]
HoutC17B_n[4] = OTEC_hsw15(TL17B_n[4],2)+MA17B_n[4]*QC17B_n[4]/MC17B_n[4]
ToutH17B_n[4] = OTEC_Tsw15(HoutH17B_n[4],1)
ToutC17B_n[4] = OTEC_Tsw15(HoutC17B_n[4],2)
Taq_out17B[4] = 0 [C]+ToutH17B_n[4]*1 [DELTAC]
Taf_out17B[4] = 0 [C]+ToutC17B_n[4]*1 [DELTAC]
TMH17B_n[4] = (TH17B_n[4]+ToutH17B_n[4])/2
TMC17B_n[4] = (TL17B_n[4]+ToutC17B_n[4])/2

{111. BOMBEAMENTO COM CAUDAIS RECALCULADOS}
RHOH17B_n[4] = OTEC_rhosw15(TH17B_n[4],1)
RHOC17B_n[4] = OTEC_rhosw15(TL17B_n[4],2)
RHOMH17B_n[4] = OTEC_rhosw15(TMH17B_n[4],1)
RHOMC17B_n[4] = OTEC_rhosw15(TMC17B_n[4],2)
MUH17B_n[4] = OTEC_muSW_tubo(TH17B_n[4],SP_aq)
MUC17B_n[4] = OTEC_muSW_tubo(TL17B_n[4],SP_af)
MUMH17B_n[4] = OTEC_muSW_tubo(TMH17B_n[4],SP_aq)
MUMC17B_n[4] = OTEC_muSW_tubo(TMC17B_n[4],SP_af)
P_tubo_aq17B[4] = OTEC_Prede11(MH17B_n[4],1/RHOH17B_n[4],N_aq_cenario,aH10_n,dH10_n,lH10_n,RHOH17B_n[4],MUH17B_n[4],epsrel_aq_tubo,eta_aq_tubo,K_aq11)*1 [kW]
P_tubo_af17B[4] = OTEC_Prede11(MC17B_n[4],1/RHOC17B_n[4],N_CWP_cenario,aC10_n,dC10_n,lC10_n,RHOC17B_n[4],MUC17B_n[4],epsrel_CWP,eta_af_tubo,K_af11)*1 [kW]
P_canal_aq17B[4] = OTEC_Pcan12(MH17B_n[4],1,Nc_ev14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,RHOMH17B_n[4],MUMH17B_n[4],beta_M_num,eta_aq_tubo)*1 [kW]
P_canal_af17B[4] = OTEC_Pcan12(MC17B_n[4],1,Nc_cond14,Aw_num,Dw_num,lp12_n,RHOMC17B_n[4],MUMC17B_n[4],beta_M_num,eta_af_tubo)*1 [kW]
DP_col17B[4] = g11*(RHOC17B_n[4]-RHOH17B_n[4])*1 [kg/m^3]*H_vertical_CWP*OTEC_colfrac11(expo_perfil11)*Convert(Pa,kPa)
P_col17B[4] = DP_col17B[4]*(m_af17B[4]/(RHOC17B_n[4]*1 [kg/m^3]))/eta_af_tubo
P_agua17B[4] = P_tubo_aq17B[4]+P_tubo_af17B[4]+P_canal_aq17B[4]+P_canal_af17B[4]+P_col17B[4]
P_ger17B[4] = m_am17B[4]*w_t17B[4]*eta_ger
P_b_am17B[4] = m_am17B[4]*w_b17B[4]/eta_m_am
P_aux17B[4] = P_agua17B[4]+P_b_am17B[4]
P_liq_antes_reserva17B[4] = P_ger17B[4]-P_aux17B[4]
P_liq17B[4] = P_liq_antes_reserva17B[4]-P_reserva14
Delta_P_ref17B[4] = P_liq17B[4]-P_liq14
Calor_ev17B[4] = m_am17B[4]*q_ev17B[4]
Calor_co17B[4] = m_am17B[4]*q_cond17B[4]
R_ciclo17B[4] = m_am17B[4]*(q_ev17B[4]-q_cond17B[4]-w_t17B[4]+w_b17B[4])
R_QH17B[4] = (MA17B_n[4]*QE17B_n[4]-MH17B_n[4]*(OTEC_hsw15(TH17B_n[4],1)-OTEC_hsw15(ToutH17B_n[4],1)))*1 [kW]
R_QC17B[4] = (MA17B_n[4]*QC17B_n[4]-MC17B_n[4]*(OTEC_hsw15(ToutC17B_n[4],2)-OTEC_hsw15(TL17B_n[4],2)))*1 [kW]

{112. ADMISSIBILIDADE SEPARADA DA CONVERGENCIA}
V_aq17B[4] = MH17B_n[4]/RHOH17B_n[4]/(N_aq_cenario*aH10_n)*1 [m/s]
V_af17B[4] = MC17B_n[4]/RHOC17B_n[4]/(N_CWP_cenario*aC10_n)*1 [m/s]
Vcan_aq17B[4] = MH17B_n[4]/(Nc_ev14*Aw_num*RHOMH17B_n[4])*1 [m/s]
Vcan_af17B[4] = MC17B_n[4]/(Nc_cond14*Aw_num*RHOMC17B_n[4])*1 [m/s]
Erro_bal17B[4] = MAX(ABS(R_ciclo17B[4]),ABS(R_QH17B[4]),ABS(R_QC17B[4]))/(1 [kW])
Margem_oper17B[4] = MIN(1-m_aq17B[4]/m_aq14,1-m_af17B[4]/m_af14,(T_ev-T_ev17B[4])/(1 [DELTAC]),(T_cond17B[4]-T_cond)/(1 [DELTAC]),(DTmin_ev17B[4]-DTpinch_ev)/(1 [DELTAC]),(DTmin_co17B[4]-DTpinch_cond)/(1 [DELTAC]),(T_ev17B[4]-T_cond17B[4])/(1 [DELTAC]),x4_17B[4],1-x4_17B[4],MA17B_n[4],MH17B_n[4],MC17B_n[4],(v_tubo_max-V_aq17B[4])/(1 [m/s]),(v_tubo_max-V_af17B[4])/(1 [m/s]),(v_canal_ref-Vcan_aq17B[4])/(1 [m/s]),(v_canal_ref-Vcan_af17B[4])/(1 [m/s]))
Admissivel17B[4] = IF(Margem_oper17B[4],(-0.00001),0,1,1)*IF(Erro_bal17B[4],0.001,1,1,0)
Fraccao_agua_quente17B[4] = m_aq17B[4]/m_aq14
Fraccao_agua_fria17B[4] = m_af17B[4]/m_af14
Dados_mensais_reais17B[4] = 0
Ean_calc17B[4] = 0



{FECHO DO ENSAIO DE REFERENCIA: AREA ACTIVA NO EVAPORADOR,
 PINCH ACTIVO NO CONDENSADOR. Verificar os restantes limites em Res_OK.}
Duplicate jp17=1,11
  MHpin17[4,jp17] = MA17B_n[4]*(OTEC_cy17(13,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1)-(jp17-1)*OTEC_cy17(14,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1)/10)/(OTEC_hsw15(TH17B_n[4],1)-OTEC_hsw15(OTEC_cy17(19,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,jp17)+PE15_n,1))
End
MHmin_n17[4] = MAX(MHpin17[4,1],MHpin17[4,2],MHpin17[4,3],MHpin17[4,4],MHpin17[4,5],MHpin17[4,6],MHpin17[4,7],MHpin17[4,8],MHpin17[4,9],MHpin17[4,10],MHpin17[4,11])
MHmin17[4] = MHmin_n17[4]*1 [kg/s]
A_ev_req17B[4] = A_ev_inst14
m_af17B[4] = MA17B_n[4]*OTEC_cy17(16,(TEs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),(TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC]),eta_t,eta_b_am,1)/(OTEC_hsw15((TCs17_4-0 [C])/(1 [DELTAC])-PC15_n,2)-OTEC_hsw15(TL17B_n[4],2))*1 [kg/s]
